+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Production <see cref="IIntegrationLaneFilter"/>. Selects the Category=Integration cases of one
+/// host by excluding the other host's Platform trait, so untagged integration cases run on both hosts.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class IntegrationLaneFilter : IIntegrationLaneFilter
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string Compose(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Linux",
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Windows",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown integration lane host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: The host the running build is on.</summary>
+    public static IntegrationLaneHost CurrentHost()
+        => OperatingSystem.IsWindows() ? IntegrationLaneHost.Windows : IntegrationLaneHost.Linux;
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Host operating system whose Category=Integration cases a MigrationIntegrationTests run selects.
+/// </summary>
+enum IntegrationLaneHost
+{
+    /// <summary>A Windows host (PAYTON-LEGION2).</summary>
+    Windows,
+
+    /// <summary>A Linux host (LAB-OMARCHY).</summary>
+    Linux,
+}
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019: Composes the dotnet test filter of the host-scoped integration lane. Cases tagged
+/// Platform=Linux run only on Linux hosts, cases tagged Platform=Windows only on Windows hosts, and untagged
+/// integration cases on both.
+/// </summary>
+interface IIntegrationLaneFilter
+{
+    /// <summary>Returns the test filter that selects the Category=Integration cases of <paramref name="host"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    string Compose(IntegrationLaneHost host);
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5: whether MigrationIntegrationTests may start its test processes, and why not.
+/// </summary>
+/// <param name="CanRun">True when the lane may run its tests.</param>
+/// <param name="FailureMessage">When <paramref name="CanRun"/> is false, the message that names the missing session; otherwise null.</param>
+sealed record IntegrationLaneReadiness(bool CanRun, string? FailureMessage);
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5: decides, before any test runs, whether the host session can run the integration lane.
+/// On Windows the lane runs in an interactive desktop session of the logged-on user; elsewhere no session
+/// requirement applies.
+/// </summary>
+interface IIntegrationLanePrecondition
+{
+    /// <summary>Evaluates the precondition for one host and one session.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    /// <param name="userInteractive">Whether the running process is in an interactive desktop session.</param>
+    IntegrationLaneReadiness Evaluate(IntegrationLaneHost host, bool userInteractive);
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5: production <see cref="IIntegrationLanePrecondition"/>. On Windows the integration lane runs
+/// only from an interactive desktop session of the logged-on user; a WSMan, SSH or service session is refused
+/// before any test runs, with a message that names the missing session. No session requirement applies elsewhere.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class InteractiveSessionPrecondition : IIntegrationLanePrecondition
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public IntegrationLaneReadiness Evaluate(IntegrationLaneHost host, bool userInteractive) =>
+        host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows && !userInteractive
+            ? new IntegrationLaneReadiness(
+                false,
+                "MigrationIntegrationTests needs an interactive desktop session on Windows (TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5): this process "
+                + "is not interactive (for example a WSMan, SSH or service session). Run the target from a console in the "
+                + "logged-on user's interactive desktop session.")
+            : new IntegrationLaneReadiness(true, null);
+}
diff --git a/build/LinuxProviderTestProvisioner.cs b/build/LinuxProviderTestProvisioner.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2649ae8c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/build/LinuxProviderTestProvisioner.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,320 @@
+using System.Data.Common;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.Globalization;
+using System.Net.Sockets;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018: Production Linux provisioning of the provider test endpoints (operator decision 2026-10-11,
+/// BUG-BASELINE-005). Each variable already set is adopted; otherwise it is loaded from the test-database env file and
+/// its dedicated qbrain-test container is started when stopped. Every endpoint must accept TCP before tests run.
+/// Actions and errors name variables, the file, containers and host:port only, never connection-string values.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class LinuxProviderTestProvisioner : IProviderTestProvisioner
+{
+    private static readonly TimeSpan ProbeTimeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2);
+    private const int ProbeAttempts = 30;
+
+    private static readonly (string Variable, string Container, int DefaultPort, bool IsSqlServer)[] Endpoints =
+    [
+        (ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable, ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer, 5432, false),
+        (ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable, ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer, 1433, true),
+    ];
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public ProviderTestProvisioningResult Provision(string envFilePath, IProviderTestHost host)
+    {
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrWhiteSpace(envFilePath);
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(host);
+        var actions = new List<string>();
+        var errors = new List<string>();
+        Dictionary<string, string>? fileValues = null;
+        var fileMissing = false;
+
+        foreach (var (variable, container, defaultPort, isSqlServer) in Endpoints)
+        {
+            var value = host.GetVariable(variable);
+            var loadedFromFile = false;
+            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
+            {
+                actions.Add("adopt " + variable + " (already set)");
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                if (fileValues is null && !fileMissing)
+                {
+                    var text = host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    if (text is null)
+                    {
+                        fileMissing = true;
+                        errors.Add("test-database env file not found: " + envFilePath);
+                    }
+                    else
+                    {
+                        fileValues = ParseEnvFile(text);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (fileMissing)
+                {
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                if (!fileValues!.TryGetValue(variable, out value) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
+                {
+                    errors.Add(variable + " is not defined in " + envFilePath);
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                host.SetVariable(variable, value);
+                actions.Add("load " + variable + " from " + envFilePath);
+                loadedFromFile = true;
+            }
+
+            var startedNow = false;
+            if (loadedFromFile)
+            {
+                var state = host.GetContainerState(container);
+                if (state == ProviderTestContainerState.Missing)
+                {
+                    errors.Add("container " + container + " not found");
+                    continue;
+                }
+
+                if (state == ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped)
+                {
+                    if (!host.StartContainer(container))
+                    {
+                        errors.Add("container " + container + " could not be started");
+                        continue;
+                    }
+
+                    actions.Add("start " + container);
+                    startedNow = true;
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (!TryReadEndpoint(value, isSqlServer, defaultPort, out var endpointHost, out var port, out var problem))
+            {
+                errors.Add(variable + " " + problem);
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            var endpoint = endpointHost + ":" + port.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
+            var attempts = startedNow ? ProbeAttempts : 1;
+            var reachable = false;
+            for (var attempt = 0; attempt < attempts && !reachable; attempt++)
+            {
+                reachable = host.CanConnect(endpointHost, port, ProbeTimeout);
+                if (!reachable && attempt + 1 < attempts)
+                {
+                    Thread.Sleep(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (reachable)
+            {
+                actions.Add("endpoint " + endpoint + " reachable");
+            }
+            else
+            {
+                errors.Add("endpoint " + endpoint + " for " + variable + " is not accepting connections");
+            }
+        }
+
+        return new ProviderTestProvisioningResult(actions, errors);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Parses KEY=VALUE lines; skips blanks and comments; strips an export prefix and matching quotes.</summary>
+    internal static Dictionary<string, string> ParseEnvFile(string text)
+    {
+        var values = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+        foreach (var raw in text.Split('\n'))
+        {
+            var line = raw.Trim();
+            if (line.Length == 0 || line.StartsWith('#'))
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            if (line.StartsWith("export ", StringComparison.Ordinal))
+            {
+                line = line["export ".Length..].TrimStart();
+            }
+
+            var equals = line.IndexOf('=', StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            if (equals <= 0)
+            {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            var key = line[..equals].Trim();
+            var value = line[(equals + 1)..].Trim();
+            if (value.Length >= 2 && (value[0] == '"' || value[0] == '\'') && value[^1] == value[0])
+            {
+                value = value[1..^1];
+            }
+
+            values[key] = value;
+        }
+
+        return values;
+    }
+
+    private static bool TryReadEndpoint(string connectionString, bool isSqlServer, int defaultPort, out string host, out int port, out string problem)
+    {
+        host = string.Empty;
+        port = defaultPort;
+        problem = string.Empty;
+        var builder = new DbConnectionStringBuilder();
+        try
+        {
+            builder.ConnectionString = connectionString;
+        }
+        catch (ArgumentException)
+        {
+            problem = "is not a valid connection string";
+            return false;
+        }
+
+        if (isSqlServer)
+        {
+            var server = FirstValue(builder, "Server", "Data Source", "Address", "Addr", "Network Address");
+            if (server is null)
+            {
+                problem = "names no server";
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            if (server.StartsWith("(localdb)", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
+            {
+                problem = "names a LocalDB server, which has no TCP endpoint on Linux";
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            if (server.StartsWith("tcp:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
+            {
+                server = server[4..];
+            }
+
+            var comma = server.IndexOf(',', StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            if (comma >= 0)
+            {
+                if (!int.TryParse(server[(comma + 1)..].Trim(), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out port))
+                {
+                    problem = "names a server with an unreadable port";
+                    return false;
+                }
+
+                server = server[..comma];
+            }
+
+            host = server.Split('\\')[0].Trim();
+        }
+        else
+        {
+            var server = FirstValue(builder, "Host", "Server");
+            if (server is null)
+            {
+                problem = "names no host";
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            host = server.Split(',')[0].Trim();
+            var portText = FirstValue(builder, "Port");
+            if (portText is not null && !int.TryParse(portText, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out port))
+            {
+                problem = "names an unreadable port";
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (host.Length == 0 || host == ".")
+        {
+            host = "localhost";
+        }
+
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    private static string? FirstValue(DbConnectionStringBuilder builder, params string[] keys)
+    {
+        foreach (var key in keys)
+        {
+            if (builder.TryGetValue(key, out var value) && value is not null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value.ToString()))
+            {
+                return value.ToString()!.Trim();
+            }
+        }
+
+        return null;
+    }
+}
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018: Real host boundary: process environment, file system, docker CLI (inspect and start only) and TCP.
+/// </summary>
+sealed class SystemProviderTestHost : IProviderTestHost
+{
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string? GetVariable(string name) => Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name);
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public void SetVariable(string name, string value) => Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(name, value);
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public string? ReadFile(string path) => File.Exists(path) ? File.ReadAllText(path) : null;
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public ProviderTestContainerState GetContainerState(string containerName)
+    {
+        var (exitCode, output) = Docker("inspect", "--format", "{{.State.Status}}", containerName);
+        if (exitCode != 0)
+        {
+            return ProviderTestContainerState.Missing;
+        }
+
+        return string.Equals(output.Trim(), "running", StringComparison.Ordinal)
+            ? ProviderTestContainerState.Running
+            : ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped;
+    }
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public bool StartContainer(string containerName)
+        => Docker("start", containerName).ExitCode == 0
+           && GetContainerState(containerName) == ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    public bool CanConnect(string host, int port, TimeSpan timeout)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            using var client = new TcpClient();
+            return client.ConnectAsync(host, port).Wait(timeout) && client.Connected;
+        }
+        catch (Exception exception) when (exception is SocketException or AggregateException)
+        {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+
+    private static (int ExitCode, string Output) Docker(params string[] arguments)
+    {
+        var start = new ProcessStartInfo("docker") { RedirectStandardOutput = true, RedirectStandardError = true, UseShellExecute = false };
+        foreach (var argument in arguments)
+        {
+            start.ArgumentList.Add(argument);
+        }
+
+        using var process = Process.Start(start) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Could not start docker.");
+        var output = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();
+        var error = process.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync();
+        if (!process.WaitForExit(TimeSpan.FromMinutes(2)))
+        {
+            process.Kill(entireProcessTree: true);
+            return (-1, string.Empty);
+        }
+
+        _ = error.GetAwaiter().GetResult();
+        return (process.ExitCode, output.GetAwaiter().GetResult());
+    }
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001, TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 / AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009:
+/// one entry of a single directory listing, as the user-profile scans read it.
+/// </summary>

+/// <param name="IsDirectory">True when the entry is a directory, or a link that resolves to one.</param>

diff --git a/src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/DirectoryTreeWalker.cs b/src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/DirectoryTreeWalker.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..66fc3ab2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/QBrainAi.Services/Services/DirectoryTreeWalker.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,99 @@
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001, TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 / AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009:
+/// walks a directory tree under the user profile breadth first without following directory links or other
+/// reparse points, and skips the contents of any directory whose listing fails with an IO or access error
+/// (an untrusted mount point, an access-denied or vanished folder), so a scan never fails on what it cannot
+/// traverse.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// A directory is yielded when its parent lists it, unless it is a link; its contents are walked only when its own
+/// listing succeeds. File links are yielded as files. Listing errors other than <see cref="IOException"/> and
+/// <see cref="UnauthorizedAccessException"/> propagate.
+/// </remarks>
+internal sealed class DirectoryTreeWalker
+{
+    private static readonly EnumerationOptions s_singleLevel = new()
+    {
+        RecurseSubdirectories = false,
+        IgnoreInaccessible = false,
+        AttributesToSkip = 0,
+        ReturnSpecialDirectories = false,
+    };
+
+    private readonly Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> _list;
+
+    /// <summary>Creates a walker that lists each directory through <paramref name="list"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="list">Lists one directory, or throws when it cannot be listed.</param>
+    internal DirectoryTreeWalker(Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(list);
+        _list = list;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The walker over the real file system.</summary>
+    internal static DirectoryTreeWalker FileSystem { get; } = new(ListFileSystem);
+
+    /// <summary>Every directory below <paramref name="root"/> that the walk reaches.</summary>
+    /// <param name="root">Directory to walk; it is listed but not yielded.</param>
+    /// <returns>The reached directories, breadth first.</returns>
+    internal IEnumerable<string> EnumerateDirectories(string root) => Walk(root, yieldDirectories: true);
+
+    /// <summary>Every file below <paramref name="root"/> that the walk reaches.</summary>
+    /// <param name="root">Directory to walk.</param>
+    /// <returns>The reached files, breadth first.</returns>
+    internal IEnumerable<string> EnumerateFiles(string root) => Walk(root, yieldDirectories: false);
+
+    /// <summary>Lists one real directory with each entry's directory and link flags.</summary>
+    /// <param name="directory">Directory to list.</param>
+    /// <returns>The entries of <paramref name="directory"/>.</returns>
+    internal static IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> ListFileSystem(string directory) =>
+        new DirectoryInfo(directory)
+            .EnumerateFileSystemInfos("*", s_singleLevel)
+            .Select(info => new DirectoryListingEntry(
+                info.FullName,
+                info is DirectoryInfo,
+                (info.Attributes & FileAttributes.ReparsePoint) != 0))
+            .ToList();
+
+    private IEnumerable<string> Walk(string root, bool yieldDirectories)
+    {
+        var pending = new Queue<string>();
+        pending.Enqueue(root);
+        while (pending.Count > 0)
+        {
+            foreach (var entry in TryList(pending.Dequeue()))
+            {
+                if (entry.IsDirectory)
+                {
+                    if (entry.IsLink)
+                        continue;
+                    pending.Enqueue(entry.Path);
+                    if (yieldDirectories)
+                        yield return entry.Path;
+                }
+                else if (!yieldDirectories)
+                {
+                    yield return entry.Path;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> TryList(string directory)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            return _list(directory);
+        }
+        catch (IOException)
+        {
+            return [];
+        }
+        catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
+        {
+            return [];
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLaneFilterContract.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLaneFilterContract.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..18c2ac4a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Build.Tests/IntegrationLaneFilterContract.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,93 @@
+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-1): Contract cases and assertions for <see cref="IIntegrationLaneFilter"/>. The expected filters
+/// come from the acceptance criterion; a small evaluator of the trait grammar the lane uses (terms joined by '&amp;',
+/// Name=Value requires the trait, Name!=Value is met by any other value or by no trait) checks what each filter
+/// selects for an untagged case, an own-host case, an other-host case and a non-integration case. The same
+/// assertions run against the scripted filter (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong filters (negative controls), and the
+/// production filter.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class IntegrationLaneFilterContract
+{
+    /// <summary>Every host name, in the order the contract checks them.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] HostNames = ["windows", "linux"];
+
+    /// <summary>Parses a contract host name.</summary>
+    /// <param name="hostName">windows or linux.</param>
+    internal static IntegrationLaneHost Parse(string hostName) => hostName switch
+    {
+        "windows" => IntegrationLaneHost.Windows,
+        "linux" => IntegrationLaneHost.Linux,
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(hostName), hostName, "Unknown contract host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>The filter TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-1 requires for <paramref name="host"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    internal static string ExpectedFilter(IntegrationLaneHost host) => host switch
+    {
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Windows => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Linux",
+        IntegrationLaneHost.Linux => "Category=Integration&Platform!=Windows",
+        _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(host), host, "Unknown host."),
+    };
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts that <paramref name="filter"/> meets the contract for the named host.</summary>
+    /// <param name="hostName">windows or linux.</param>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertHost(string hostName, IIntegrationLaneFilter filter)
+    {
+        var host = Parse(hostName);
+        var actual = filter.Compose(host);
+
+        Assert.Equal(ExpectedFilter(host), actual);
+        AssertSelection(host, actual);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts what <paramref name="filter"/> selects on <paramref name="host"/>: untagged and own-host integration
+    /// cases run; other-host and non-integration cases do not. Independent of the exact filter text, so the negative
+    /// controls prove each of these assertions rejects a wrong filter on its own.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the lane runs on.</param>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter text under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertSelection(IntegrationLaneHost host, string filter)
+    {
+        var own = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Windows" : "Linux";
+        var other = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Linux" : "Windows";
+
+        Assert.True(Selects(filter, category: "Integration", platform: null), "An untagged integration case must run on every host.");
+        Assert.True(Selects(filter, category: "Integration", platform: own), $"A Platform={own} integration case must run on its own host.");
+        Assert.False(Selects(filter, category: "Integration", platform: other), $"A Platform={other} integration case must not run on a {own} host.");
+        Assert.False(Selects(filter, category: null, platform: null), "A case without Category=Integration must not run in the integration lane.");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Evaluates the '&amp;'-joined Name=Value and Name!=Value terms against one case's traits.</summary>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter text.</param>
+    /// <param name="category">The case's Category trait, or null.</param>
+    /// <param name="platform">The case's Platform trait, or null.</param>
+    internal static bool Selects(string filter, string? category, string? platform)
+    {
+        var traits = new Dictionary<string, string?>(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["Category"] = category,
+            ["Platform"] = platform,
+        };
+        foreach (var term in filter.Split('&', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries))
+        {
+            var negated = term.Contains("!=", StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            var parts = term.Split(negated ? "!=" : "=", 2, StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
+            if (parts.Length != 2 || !traits.TryGetValue(parts[0], out var value))
+            {
+                throw new FormatException($"Unsupported filter term '{term}'.");
+            }
+
+            var equal = string.Equals(value, parts[1], StringComparison.Ordinal);
+            if (negated ? equal : !equal)
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+        }
+
+        return true;
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-1), BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="IntegrationLaneFilterContract"/> assertions accept a
+/// scripted filter that produces the contract-derived filters, and reject deliberately wrong filters (negative
+/// controls), before any production filter code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class IntegrationLaneFilterMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: every host passes against the scripted filter.</summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host name from <see cref="IntegrationLaneFilterContract.HostNames"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows")]
+    [InlineData("linux")]
+    public void ScriptedFilter_PassesHost(string host)
+        => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.AssertHost(host, new ScriptedFilter(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the theory above covers every contract host.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedFilterTheory_CoversEveryHost()
+        => Assert.Equal(2, IntegrationLaneFilterContract.HostNames.Length);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls: each wrong filter is rejected (the other host's cases admitted, untagged
+    /// cases dropped, hosts swapped, the Category=Integration term dropped).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the fault is checked on.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    public void WrongFilter_IsRejected(string host, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.AssertHost(host, new ScriptedFilter(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 negative controls: the selection assertions alone, without the exact-text check, reject each
+    /// wrong filter. Each fault breaks a different assertion (other host admitted, untagged dropped, own host
+    /// excluded, non-integration admitted), so every selection assertion is shown to discriminate.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="host">Host the fault is checked on.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.AdmitsOtherHost)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsUntagged)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.SwapsHosts)]
+    [InlineData("windows", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    [InlineData("linux", Fault.DropsCategory)]
+    public void WrongFilter_FailsSelection(string host, Fault fault)
+    {
+        var parsed = IntegrationLaneFilterContract.Parse(host);
+        var filter = new ScriptedFilter(fault).Compose(parsed);
+        Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(() => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.AssertSelection(parsed, filter));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019: the contract evaluator refuses a term it cannot read instead of guessing.</summary>
+    /// <param name="filter">Filter with an unsupported term.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("Category~Integration")]
+    [InlineData("Category=Integration&Owner!=Linux")]
+    public void Evaluator_RejectsUnsupportedTerm(string filter)
+        => Assert.Throws<FormatException>(() => IntegrationLaneFilterContract.Selects(filter, "Integration", null));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted filter for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the contract-derived filter.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects every integration case, including the other host's.</summary>
+        AdmitsOtherHost,
+
+        /// <summary>Selects only cases tagged for the host, dropping untagged integration cases.</summary>
+        DropsUntagged,
+
+        /// <summary>Excludes the host's own cases instead of the other host's.</summary>
+        SwapsHosts,
+
+        /// <summary>Drops the Category=Integration term.</summary>
+        DropsCategory,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted filter: the contract-derived filters, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedFilter(Fault fault) : IIntegrationLaneFilter
+    {
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string Compose(IntegrationLaneHost host)
+        {
+            var own = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Windows" : "Linux";
+            var other = host == IntegrationLaneHost.Windows ? "Linux" : "Windows";
+            return fault switch
+            {
+                Fault.None => $"Category=Integration&Platform!={other}",
+                Fault.AdmitsOtherHost => "Category=Integration",
+                Fault.DropsUntagged => $"Category=Integration&Platform={own}",
+                Fault.SwapsHosts => $"Category=Integration&Platform!={own}",
+                Fault.DropsCategory => $"Platform!={other}",
+                _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(fault), fault, "Unknown fault."),
+            };
+        }
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-5): contract scenarios and assertions for <see cref="IIntegrationLanePrecondition"/>. The
+/// expected outcomes come from the criterion: on Windows the lane runs only from an interactive desktop session
+/// and otherwise fails before any test, naming the missing session; on Linux no session requirement applies. The
+/// same assertions run against the scripted precondition (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong preconditions (negative
+/// controls), and the production precondition.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class IntegrationLanePreconditionContract
+{
+    /// <summary>Every scenario name, in the order the contract checks them.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] ScenarioNames =
+    [
+        "windows-interactive",
+        "windows-noninteractive",
+        "linux-interactive",
+        "linux-noninteractive",
+    ];
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts that <paramref name="precondition"/> meets the named scenario.</summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario name from <see cref="ScenarioNames"/>.</param>
+    /// <param name="precondition">Precondition under test.</param>
+    internal static void AssertScenario(string scenario, IIntegrationLanePrecondition precondition)
+    {
+        var (host, interactive, mayRun) = scenario switch
+        {
+            "windows-interactive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Windows, true, true),
+            "windows-noninteractive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Windows, false, false),
+            "linux-interactive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Linux, true, true),
+            "linux-noninteractive" => (IntegrationLaneHost.Linux, false, true),
+            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(scenario), scenario, "Unknown contract scenario."),
+        };
+
+        var readiness = precondition.Evaluate(host, interactive);
+
+        Assert.Equal(mayRun, readiness.CanRun);
+        if (mayRun)
+        {
+            Assert.Null(readiness.FailureMessage);
+            return;
+        }
+
+        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(readiness.FailureMessage), "A lane that cannot run must say why instead of stopping silently.");
+        Assert.Contains("interactive desktop session", readiness.FailureMessage, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.Contains("MigrationIntegrationTests", readiness.FailureMessage, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+}

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 (AC-5), BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="IntegrationLanePreconditionContract"/> assertions
+/// accept a scripted precondition that produces the criterion's outcomes, and reject deliberately wrong
+/// preconditions (negative controls), before any production precondition code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class IntegrationLanePreconditionMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5: every scenario passes against the scripted precondition.</summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario name from <see cref="IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.ScenarioNames"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows-interactive")]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive")]
+    [InlineData("linux-interactive")]
+    [InlineData("linux-noninteractive")]
+    public void ScriptedPrecondition_PassesScenario(string scenario)
+        => IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedPrecondition(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5: the theory above covers every contract scenario.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryScenario()
+        => Assert.Equal(4, IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.ScenarioNames.Length);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-5 negative controls: a precondition that runs a non-interactive Windows session, stops
+    /// without a message (a silent skip), names no session in its message, or blocks a Linux host, is rejected.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario the fault breaks.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive", Fault.RunsWhenNonInteractive)]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive", Fault.StopsSilently)]
+    [InlineData("windows-noninteractive", Fault.MessageOmitsSession)]
+    [InlineData("linux-noninteractive", Fault.BlocksLinux)]
+    public void WrongPrecondition_IsRejected(string scenario, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => IntegrationLanePreconditionContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedPrecondition(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted precondition for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the criterion's outcomes.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Lets a non-interactive Windows session run the lane.</summary>
+        RunsWhenNonInteractive,
+
+        /// <summary>Refuses the lane but gives no message, so the run would stop silently.</summary>
+        StopsSilently,
+
+        /// <summary>Refuses the lane with a message that does not name the missing session.</summary>
+        MessageOmitsSession,
+
+        /// <summary>Applies the Windows session requirement to a Linux host.</summary>
+        BlocksLinux,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted precondition: the criterion's outcomes, with an optional injected fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedPrecondition(Fault fault) : IIntegrationLanePrecondition
+    {
+        private const string Message = "MigrationIntegrationTests needs an interactive desktop session on Windows.";
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public IntegrationLaneReadiness Evaluate(IntegrationLaneHost host, bool userInteractive)
+        {
+            if (fault == Fault.BlocksLinux && !userInteractive)
+                return new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, Message);
+            if (host != IntegrationLaneHost.Windows || userInteractive)
+                return new IntegrationLaneReadiness(true, null);
+
+            return fault switch
+            {
+                Fault.RunsWhenNonInteractive => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(true, null),
+                Fault.StopsSilently => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, null),
+                Fault.MessageOmitsSession => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, "MigrationIntegrationTests cannot run here."),
+                _ => new IntegrationLaneReadiness(false, Message),
+            };
+        }
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningContract.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningContract.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..fef74b60
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningContract.cs

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018 (AC-1 to AC-4, AC-6): Contract scenarios and assertions for <see cref="IProviderTestProvisioner"/>.
+/// A recording fake host stands in for the process environment, the test-database env file, Docker and TCP. The
+/// expectations come from the acceptance criteria; the fixture secrets are fake markers that must never appear in
+/// actions or errors. The same assertions run against the scripted mock (BDPv4 mock gate), wrong mocks (negative
+/// controls), and the production provisioner.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class ProviderTestProvisioningContract
+{
+    /// <summary>Every scenario name, in the order the contract checks them.</summary>
+    internal static readonly string[] ScenarioNames =
+        ["load-from-file", "adopt-set", "file-missing", "variable-missing-in-file", "stopped-container", "missing-container", "unreachable", "localdb-on-linux"];
+
+    /// <summary>Env file path used by every scenario.</summary>
+    internal const string EnvFile = "/home/fixture/.config/qbrain-test/test-db.env";
+
+    /// <summary>Fake PostgreSQL secret marker.</summary>
+    internal const string PostgresSecret = "PG_SECRET_VALUE_7";
+
+    /// <summary>Fake SQL Server secret marker.</summary>
+    internal const string SqlServerSecret = "SQL_SECRET_VALUE_9";
+
+    /// <summary>PostgreSQL connection string in the env file.</summary>
+    internal const string PostgresValue = "Host=localhost;Port=55432;Username=tester;Password=" + PostgresSecret + ";Database=postgres";
+
+    /// <summary>SQL Server connection string in the env file.</summary>
+    internal const string SqlServerValue = "Server=localhost,51433;User ID=sa;Password=" + SqlServerSecret + ";TrustServerCertificate=True";
+
+    /// <summary>Env file defining both variables, with a comment, an export prefix and a quoted value.</summary>
+    internal const string FullEnvFile =
+        "# QBrain.AI provider test servers\n"
+        + "export " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + "=\"" + PostgresValue + "\"\n"
+        + "\n"
+        + ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable + "=" + SqlServerValue + "\n";
+
+    /// <summary>Builds the fake host of a named scenario.</summary>
+    internal static FakeProviderTestHost CreateHost(string scenario)
+    {
+        var host = new FakeProviderTestHost(scenario);
+        host.Containers[ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+        host.Containers[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+        host.Containers["sql-server-developer"] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+        host.Files[EnvFile] = FullEnvFile;
+        switch (scenario)
+        {
+            case "load-from-file":
+            case "unreachable":
+                if (scenario == "unreachable")
+                {
+                    host.Unreachable.Add("localhost:51433");
+                }
+
+                break;
+            case "adopt-set":
+                host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable] = "Host=db.example;Port=6543;Password=" + PostgresSecret;
+                host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable] = "Server=tcp:sql.example,1444;Password=" + SqlServerSecret;
+                host.Files.Remove(EnvFile);
+                break;
+            case "file-missing":
+                host.Files.Remove(EnvFile);
+                break;
+            case "variable-missing-in-file":
+                host.Files[EnvFile] = ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + "=" + PostgresValue + "\n";
+                break;
+            case "stopped-container":
+                host.Containers[ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer] = ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped;
+                break;
+            case "missing-container":
+                host.Containers.Remove(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer);
+                break;
+            case "localdb-on-linux":
+                host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable] = "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Integrated Security=True";
+                break;
+            default:
+                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown scenario " + scenario);
+        }
+
+        return host;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Runs one scenario against the provisioner and asserts its contract.</summary>
+    internal static void AssertScenario(string scenario, IProviderTestProvisioner provisioner)
+    {
+        var host = CreateHost(scenario);
+        var result = provisioner.Provision(EnvFile, host);
+        var text = string.Join("\n", result.Actions.Concat(result.Errors));
+
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(PostgresSecret, text, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.DoesNotContain(SqlServerSecret, text, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.All(host.ContainerCalls, name => Assert.StartsWith("qbrain-test-", name, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+
+        switch (scenario)
+        {
+            case "load-from-file":
+                Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("; ", result.Errors));
+                Assert.Equal(PostgresValue, host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable]);
+                Assert.Equal(SqlServerValue, host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable]);
+                Assert.Empty(host.Started);
+                Assert.Contains("localhost:55432", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("localhost:51433", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("load " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + " from " + EnvFile, result.Actions);
+                Assert.Contains("load " + ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable + " from " + EnvFile, result.Actions);
+                break;
+            case "adopt-set":
+                Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("; ", result.Errors));
+                Assert.Equal(0, host.ReadFileCalls);
+                Assert.Empty(host.ContainerCalls);
+                Assert.Empty(host.SetCalls);
+                Assert.Contains("db.example:6543", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("sql.example:1444", host.Probes);
+                Assert.Contains("adopt " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable + " (already set)", result.Actions);
+                break;
+            case "file-missing":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(EnvFile, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                Assert.Empty(host.SetCalls);
+                Assert.Empty(host.Probes);
+                break;
+            case "variable-missing-in-file":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable, StringComparison.Ordinal) && error.Contains(EnvFile, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                Assert.Equal(PostgresValue, host.Variables[ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable]);
+                break;
+            case "stopped-container":
+                Assert.True(result.Succeeded, string.Join("; ", result.Errors));
+                Assert.Equal(new[] { ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer }, host.Started);
+                Assert.Contains("start " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer, result.Actions);
+                break;
+            case "missing-container":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer, StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                Assert.DoesNotContain(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer, host.Started);
+                break;
+            case "unreachable":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains("localhost:51433", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                break;
+            case "localdb-on-linux":
+                Assert.False(result.Succeeded);
+                Assert.Contains(result.Errors, error => error.Contains(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable, StringComparison.Ordinal) && error.Contains("LocalDB", StringComparison.Ordinal));
+                break;
+            default:
+                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown scenario " + scenario);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Recording fake of <see cref="IProviderTestHost"/>.</summary>
+    internal sealed class FakeProviderTestHost(string scenario) : IProviderTestHost
+    {
+        /// <summary>Scenario name (read by the scripted mock only).</summary>
+        public string Scenario { get; } = scenario;
+
+        /// <summary>Process environment.</summary>
+        public Dictionary<string, string> Variables { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Files by path.</summary>
+        public Dictionary<string, string> Files { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Container states by name.</summary>
+        public Dictionary<string, ProviderTestContainerState> Containers { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>host:port endpoints that refuse connections.</summary>
+        public HashSet<string> Unreachable { get; } = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Variables set through the boundary.</summary>
+        public List<string> SetCalls { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Container names passed to GetContainerState or StartContainer.</summary>
+        public List<string> ContainerCalls { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Containers started.</summary>
+        public List<string> Started { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>host:port endpoints probed.</summary>
+        public List<string> Probes { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Number of file reads.</summary>
+        public int ReadFileCalls { get; private set; }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string? GetVariable(string name) => Variables.TryGetValue(name, out var value) ? value : null;
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public void SetVariable(string name, string value)
+        {
+            SetCalls.Add(name);
+            Variables[name] = value;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public string? ReadFile(string path)
+        {
+            ReadFileCalls++;
+            return Files.TryGetValue(path, out var text) ? text : null;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public ProviderTestContainerState GetContainerState(string containerName)
+        {
+            ContainerCalls.Add(containerName);
+            return Containers.TryGetValue(containerName, out var state) ? state : ProviderTestContainerState.Missing;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public bool StartContainer(string containerName)
+        {
+            ContainerCalls.Add(containerName);
+            if (!Containers.TryGetValue(containerName, out var state) || state == ProviderTestContainerState.Missing)
+            {
+                return false;
+            }
+
+            Started.Add(containerName);
+            Containers[containerName] = ProviderTestContainerState.Running;
+            return true;
+        }
+
+        /// <inheritdoc />
+        public bool CanConnect(string host, int port, TimeSpan timeout)
+        {
+            var endpoint = host + ":" + port.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
+            Probes.Add(endpoint);
+            return !Unreachable.Contains(endpoint);
+        }
+    }
+}
diff --git a/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningMockGateTests.cs b/tests/Build.Tests/ProviderTestProvisioningMockGateTests.cs

index 00000000..281a8b2c

+namespace NukeBuild.Tests;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-018 (AC-1 to AC-4), BDPv4 mock gate: proves the <see cref="ProviderTestProvisioningContract"/>
+/// assertions accept a scripted provisioner that produces the contract-derived effects, and reject deliberately wrong
+/// provisioners (negative controls), before any production provisioning code is written.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class ProviderTestProvisioningMockGateTests
+{
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-018: every scenario passes against the scripted provisioner.</summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario name from <see cref="ProviderTestProvisioningContract.ScenarioNames"/>.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("load-from-file")]
+    [InlineData("adopt-set")]
+    [InlineData("file-missing")]
+    [InlineData("variable-missing-in-file")]
+    [InlineData("stopped-container")]
+    [InlineData("missing-container")]
+    [InlineData("unreachable")]
+    [InlineData("localdb-on-linux")]
+    public void ScriptedProvisioner_PassesScenario(string scenario)
+        => ProviderTestProvisioningContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedProvisioner(Fault.None));
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-NUKE-018: the theory above covers every contract scenario.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedProvisionerTheory_CoversEveryScenario()
+        => Assert.Equal(8, ProviderTestProvisioningContract.ScenarioNames.Length);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-NUKE-018 negative controls: each wrong provisioner is rejected by the scenario it violates
+    /// (AC-4 value leak, AC-2 foreign container, AC-1 file read although set, AC-3 missing file ignored,
+    /// AC-3 probe skipped).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="scenario">Scenario the fault violates.</param>
+    /// <param name="fault">Injected fault.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("load-from-file", Fault.LeaksValue)]
+    [InlineData("load-from-file", Fault.TouchesOtherContainer)]
+    [InlineData("adopt-set", Fault.ReadsFileWhenSet)]
+    [InlineData("file-missing", Fault.IgnoresMissingFile)]
+    [InlineData("unreachable", Fault.SkipsProbe)]
+    public void WrongProvisioner_IsRejected(string scenario, Fault fault)
+        => Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => ProviderTestProvisioningContract.AssertScenario(scenario, new ScriptedProvisioner(fault)));
+
+    /// <summary>Faults injected into the scripted provisioner for negative controls.</summary>
+    public enum Fault
+    {
+        /// <summary>No fault: the contract-derived behavior.</summary>
+        None,
+
+        /// <summary>Writes a connection-string value into an action.</summary>
+        LeaksValue,
+
+        /// <summary>Inspects a container other than the two qbrain-test containers.</summary>
+        TouchesOtherContainer,
+
+        /// <summary>Reads the env file although both variables are set.</summary>
+        ReadsFileWhenSet,
+
+        /// <summary>Reports no error when the env file is missing.</summary>
+        IgnoresMissingFile,
+
+        /// <summary>Reports success without probing the endpoints.</summary>
+        SkipsProbe,
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Scripted mock: produces the expected effects of the fake host's scenario, with one optional fault.</summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedProvisioner(Fault fault) : IProviderTestProvisioner
+    {
+        public ProviderTestProvisioningResult Provision(string envFilePath, IProviderTestHost host)
+        {
+            var fake = (ProviderTestProvisioningContract.FakeProviderTestHost)host;
+            var actions = new List<string>();
+            var errors = new List<string>();
+            const string pg = ProviderTestNames.PostgresVariable;
+            const string sql = ProviderTestNames.SqlServerVariable;
+            switch (fake.Scenario)
+            {
+                case "load-from-file":
+                case "unreachable":
+                case "stopped-container":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    host.SetVariable(sql, ProviderTestContractValues.SqlServer);
+                    actions.Add("load " + pg + " from " + envFilePath);
+                    actions.Add("load " + sql + " from " + envFilePath);
+                    if (fake.Scenario == "stopped-container" && host.GetContainerState(ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer) == ProviderTestContainerState.Stopped)
+                    {
+                        host.StartContainer(ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer);
+                        actions.Add("start " + ProviderTestNames.PostgresContainer);
+                    }
+
+                    if (fault == Fault.TouchesOtherContainer)
+                    {
+                        host.GetContainerState("sql-server-developer");
+                    }
+
+                    if (fault == Fault.LeaksValue)
+                    {
+                        actions.Add("loaded value " + ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    }
+
+                    if (fault != Fault.SkipsProbe)
+                    {
+                        var pgUp = host.CanConnect("localhost", 55432, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                        var sqlUp = host.CanConnect("localhost", 51433, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                        if (!pgUp)
+                        {
+                            errors.Add("endpoint localhost:55432 for " + pg + " is not accepting connections");
+                        }
+
+                        if (!sqlUp)
+                        {
+                            errors.Add("endpoint localhost:51433 for " + sql + " is not accepting connections");
+                        }
+                    }
+
+                    break;
+                case "adopt-set":
+                    if (fault == Fault.ReadsFileWhenSet)
+                    {
+                        host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    }
+
+                    actions.Add("adopt " + pg + " (already set)");
+                    actions.Add("adopt " + sql + " (already set)");
+                    host.CanConnect("db.example", 6543, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                    host.CanConnect("sql.example", 1444, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
+                    break;
+                case "file-missing":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    if (fault != Fault.IgnoresMissingFile)
+                    {
+                        errors.Add("test-database env file not found: " + envFilePath);
+                    }
+
+                    break;
+                case "variable-missing-in-file":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    errors.Add(sql + " is not defined in " + envFilePath);
+                    break;
+                case "missing-container":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    host.SetVariable(sql, ProviderTestContractValues.SqlServer);
+                    host.GetContainerState(ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer);
+                    errors.Add("container " + ProviderTestNames.SqlServerContainer + " not found");
+                    break;
+                case "localdb-on-linux":
+                    host.ReadFile(envFilePath);
+                    host.SetVariable(pg, ProviderTestContractValues.Postgres);
+                    errors.Add(sql + " names a LocalDB server, which has no TCP endpoint on Linux");
+                    break;
+                default:
+                    throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown scenario " + fake.Scenario);
+            }
+
+            return new ProviderTestProvisioningResult(actions, errors);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Contract values the scripted mock sets.</summary>
+    private static class ProviderTestContractValues
+    {
+        public const string Postgres = ProviderTestProvisioningContract.PostgresValue;
+        public const string SqlServer = ProviderTestProvisioningContract.SqlServerValue;
+    }
+}

+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-QBRAIN-005: the legacy McpServer:McpAgent section binds through the options validator, which
+/// requires a workspace path that is fully qualified on the running host. A fully qualified path differs
+/// by host, so this is an integration test on every host (TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006: no unit test depends
+/// on the platform).
+/// </summary>
+[Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+public sealed class McpAgentOptionsLegacySectionHostTests
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-QBRAIN-005: McpServer:McpAgent still binds when the canonical section is absent.
+    /// An in-memory configuration supplies the legacy section only, with a host-native workspace path.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void AddQBrainAiMcpAgent_BindsLegacyMcpServerSection()
+    {
+        var workspacePath = OperatingSystem.IsWindows() ? @"E:\github\McpServer" : "/var/lib/McpServer";
+        var configuration = new ConfigurationBuilder().AddInMemoryCollection(new Dictionary<string, string?>
+        {
+            ["McpServer:McpAgent:ApiKey"] = "legacy-token",
+            ["McpServer:McpAgent:WorkspacePath"] = workspacePath,
+        }).Build();
+        var services = new ServiceCollection();
+        services.AddSingleton<IConfiguration>(configuration);
+        services.AddQBrainAiMcpAgent();
+
+        using var provider = services.BuildServiceProvider();
+        var options = provider.GetRequiredService<IOptions<McpAgentOptions>>().Value;
+
+        Assert.Equal("legacy-token", options.ApiKey);
+        Assert.Equal(workspacePath, options.WorkspacePath);
+    }

+    {

+using QBrainAi.Tests.Shared;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Configuration;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008), BDPv4 mock gate for the trait policy of
+/// <see cref="TestRepositoryRoot"/>, the one repository-root locator: scripted trait sets (mock data) pass only when
+/// they hold <c>Category=Integration</c>, matching the scanner's ordinal trait test; negative controls show the shared
+/// assertion rejects a policy that always passes, one that always refuses, and one that refuses with another reason.
+/// The ambient case shows a unit test is refused before the checkout is touched; the integration case is the
+/// production binding and reads the checkout.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class TestRepositoryRootPolicyTests
+{
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>? Traits, bool Allowed)> Cases =
+        new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["integration"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Integration"])), true),
+            ["integration-with-platform"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Integration"]), ("Platform", ["Linux"])), true),
+            ["integration-among-categories"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Slow", "Integration"])), true),
+            ["unit-category"] = (Traits(("Category", ["Unit"])), false),
+            ["no-traits"] = (Traits(), false),
+            ["no-test-context"] = (null, false),
+            ["platform-only"] = (Traits(("Platform", ["Windows"])), false),
+            ["lowercase-key"] = (Traits(("category", ["Integration"])), false),
+            ["lowercase-value"] = (Traits(("Category", ["integration"])), false),
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>Every scripted trait set is allowed or refused as the rule requires.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("integration")]
+    [InlineData("integration-with-platform")]
+    [InlineData("integration-among-categories")]
+    [InlineData("unit-category")]
+    [InlineData("no-traits")]
+    [InlineData("no-test-context")]
+    [InlineData("platform-only")]
+    [InlineData("lowercase-key")]
+    [InlineData("lowercase-value")]
+    public void ScriptedTraits_AreAllowedOnlyForIntegration(string name)
+    {
+        var (traits, allowed) = Cases[name];
+        AssertPolicy(TestRepositoryRoot.EnsureIntegration, traits, allowed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The theory above covers every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(9, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Negative controls: the shared assertion rejects a policy that lets a unit test through, one that refuses an
+    /// integration test, and one that refuses a unit test for a reason other than the rule.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void PolicyAssertionNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        var unit = Cases["unit-category"].Traits;
+        var integration = Cases["integration"].Traits;
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(_ => { }, unit, allowed: false)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(_ => throw new InvalidOperationException("refused"), integration, allowed: true)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(_ => throw new InvalidOperationException("another reason"), unit, allowed: false)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(TestRepositoryRoot.EnsureIntegration, unit, allowed: false)));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertPolicy(TestRepositoryRoot.EnsureIntegration, integration, allowed: true)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A unit test that reaches the locator is refused before the checkout is located or read.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Resolve_InAUnitTest_IsRefusedWithTheRule()
+    {
+        var resolve = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => TestRepositoryRoot.Resolve());
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", resolve.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        var combine = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests"));
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", combine.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        var metadata = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => TestRepositoryRoot.BuildMetadataRoot);
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", metadata.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Production binding: in an integration test the locator returns the checkout root.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public void Resolve_InAnIntegrationTest_ReturnsTheCheckoutRoot()
+    {
+        var root = TestRepositoryRoot.Resolve();
+        Assert.True(File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "QBrainAi.sln")), root);
+        Assert.Equal(Path.Combine(root, "tests"), TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests"));
+        Assert.True(File.Exists(TestRepositoryRoot.Combine("tests", "Shared", "TestRepositoryRoot.cs")));
+        Assert.False(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(TestRepositoryRoot.BuildMetadataRoot));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Assertion shared by the scripted cases and the negative controls.</summary>
+    /// <param name="policy">The policy under test.</param>
+    /// <param name="traits">Scripted traits.</param>
+    /// <param name="allowed">Whether the rule allows them.</param>
+    private static void AssertPolicy(
+        Action<IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>?> policy,
+        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>? traits,
+        bool allowed)
+    {
+        if (allowed)
+        {
+            Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => policy(traits)));
+            return;
+        }
+
+        var refusal = Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => policy(traits));
+        Assert.Contains("may not read the repository checkout", refusal.Message, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+    }
+
+    private static IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>> Traits(params (string Name, string[] Values)[] traits) =>
+        traits.ToDictionary(trait => trait.Name, trait => (IReadOnlyCollection<string>)trait.Values, StringComparer.Ordinal);
+}

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Configuration/UnitSuitePlatformScanner.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Configuration/UnitSuitePlatformScanner.cs
new file mode 100644
