+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the directory walk that both user-profile scans use (marker agent-plugin version resolution and the
+/// session-log agent-history scan): a walk never follows a directory link or other reparse point, skips the
+/// contents of any directory whose listing fails with an IO or access error, and keeps walking the rest.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 and TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 /
+/// AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009 (operator 2026-10-20: "Fix scans in PR 87 (Recommended)"; triage
+/// report b0e41c36). Test data: scripted in-memory directory trees (<see cref="ScriptedDirectoryTree"/>) whose
+/// listings return fixed entries or throw the exceptions observed on PAYTON-LEGION2 as kingd (an untrusted mount
+/// point), an access-denied folder, and a vanished folder; no file system is read. Expected outputs are derived
+/// from the acceptance criteria: a directory is yielded when its parent lists it unless it is a link, its contents
+/// are walked only when its own listing succeeds, and file links are yielded as files. Negative controls run
+/// deliberately wrong walks (one that follows links, one that propagates listing errors as the legacy recursive
+/// enumeration did, one that stops at the first error, one that drops an unlistable directory) over the same trees
+/// and prove the walk assertion rejects each.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class DirectoryTreeWalkerTests
+{
+    private const string UntrustedMountPointMessage =
+        "The path cannot be traversed because it contains an untrusted mount point.";
+
+    /// <summary>A walk under test: lists through <c>list</c> from <c>root</c> and returns what it yielded.</summary>
+    private delegate (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) WalkFunction(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list,
+        string root);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry DirEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: false);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry DirLinkEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: true);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry FileEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: false, IsLink: false);
+
+    private static DirectoryListingEntry FileLinkEntry(string path) => new(path, IsDirectory: false, IsLink: true);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted cases: the tree, the walk root, and the directories, files and listed directories the acceptance
+    /// criteria require. Each case builds a fresh tree so the listing record starts empty.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (Func<ScriptedDirectoryTree> Tree, string Root, string[] Directories, string[] Files, string[] Listed)> Cases =
+        new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["nested-tree"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"), FileEntry("/p/f1"))
+                    .Lists("/p/a", DirEntry("/p/a/b"), FileEntry("/p/a/f2"))
+                    .Lists("/p/a/b", FileEntry("/p/a/b/f3")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/a", "/p/a/b"],
+                ["/p/f1", "/p/a/f2", "/p/a/b/f3"],
+                ["/p", "/p/a", "/p/a/b"]),
+            ["untrusted-mount-point"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/bad"), DirEntry("/p/good"))
+                    .Throws("/p/bad", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage))
+                    .Lists("/p/good", DirEntry("/p/good/inner"), FileEntry("/p/good/g.md"))
+                    .Lists("/p/good/inner", FileEntry("/p/good/inner/i.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/bad", "/p/good", "/p/good/inner"],
+                ["/p/good/g.md", "/p/good/inner/i.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/bad", "/p/good", "/p/good/inner"]),
+            ["access-denied"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/locked"), DirEntry("/p/open"))
+                    .Throws("/p/locked", () => new UnauthorizedAccessException("Access to the path is denied."))
+                    .Lists("/p/open", FileEntry("/p/open/o.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/locked", "/p/open"],
+                ["/p/open/o.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/locked", "/p/open"]),
+            ["vanished-directory"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/gone"), DirEntry("/p/here"))
+                    .Throws("/p/gone", () => new DirectoryNotFoundException("Could not find a part of the path."))
+                    .Lists("/p/here", FileEntry("/p/here/h.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/gone", "/p/here"],
+                ["/p/here/h.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/gone", "/p/here"]),
+            ["directory-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/real"), DirLinkEntry("/p/link"), FileEntry("/p/f"))
+                    .Lists("/p/real", FileEntry("/p/real/x.md"))
+                    .Lists("/p/link", FileEntry("/p/link/y.md")),
+                "/p",
+                ["/p/real"],
+                ["/p/f", "/p/real/x.md"],
+                ["/p", "/p/real"]),
+            ["file-link"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Lists("/p", FileLinkEntry("/p/linked.md"), FileEntry("/p/plain.md")),
+                "/p",
+                [],
+                ["/p/linked.md", "/p/plain.md"],
+                ["/p"]),
+            ["root-unlistable"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+                    .Throws("/p", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage)),
+                "/p",
+                [],
+                [],
+                ["/p"]),
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted case names, for the theories.</summary>
+    public static TheoryData<string> CaseNames => new(Cases.Keys.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts one walk result against a case: the yielded directories, the yielded files, and the directories
+    /// the walk listed, each compared as an ordinal set (the acceptance criteria fix membership, not order).
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertWalk(
+        (string[] Directories, string[] Files, string[] Listed) expected,
+        IEnumerable<string> directories,
+        IEnumerable<string> files,
+        IEnumerable<string> listed)
+    {
+        Assert.Equal(expected.Directories.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal), directories.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+        Assert.Equal(expected.Files.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal), files.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+        Assert.Equal(
+            expected.Listed.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal),
+            listed.Distinct(StringComparer.Ordinal).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Runs one walk over a fresh copy of a case's tree and asserts its result.</summary>
+    private static void RunAndAssert(string name, WalkFunction walk)
+    {
+        var (treeFactory, root, directories, files, listed) = Cases[name];
+        var tree = treeFactory();
+        var result = walk(tree.List, root);
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), result.Directories, result.Files, tree.Listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate positive control: each case's required output passes the walk assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_RequiredOutput_PassesTheWalkAssertion(string name)
+    {
+        var (_, _, directories, files, listed) = Cases[name];
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), directories, files, listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>The theories cover every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(7, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The scripted tree is itself correct: it records every listing in order, returns the scripted entries,
+    /// throws the scripted exception, and refuses a directory it was not given, so a walk that lists a link
+    /// it should have skipped fails loudly.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTree_RecordsListingsAndRefusesUnscriptedDirectories()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"))
+            .Throws("/p/a", () => new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage));
+
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { DirEntry("/p/a") }, tree.List("/p"));
+        Assert.Equal(UntrustedMountPointMessage, Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => tree.List("/p/a")).Message);
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => tree.List("/p/unscripted"));
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { "/p", "/p/a", "/p/unscripted" }, tree.Listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: each wrong walk is rejected by the walk assertion on the case that exposes it,
+    /// inside a passing harness. The legacy recursive enumeration (follows links, propagates listing errors) fails
+    /// on the untrusted mount point, the access-denied folder, the vanished folder, the unlistable root and the
+    /// directory link; a walk that follows links fails on the directory link; a walk that stops at the first
+    /// listing error loses the healthy sibling; a walk that drops an unlistable directory loses its entry.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WrongWalkNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "untrusted-mount-point", "access-denied", "vanished-directory", "root-unlistable", "directory-link" })
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert(name, LegacyRecursiveWalk)));
+
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("directory-link", FollowsLinksWalk)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("untrusted-mount-point", StopsAtFirstErrorWalk)));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("untrusted-mount-point", DropsUnlistableDirectoryWalk)));
+
+        // Each wrong walk is wrong only where a case exposes it: on the nested tree, which has no link and no
+        // listing error, every one of them yields the required output, so the rejections above come from the
+        // behavior under test and not from an assertion that rejects everything.
+        foreach (var walk in new WalkFunction[] { LegacyRecursiveWalk, FollowsLinksWalk, StopsAtFirstErrorWalk, DropsUnlistableDirectoryWalk })
+            Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert("nested-tree", walk)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative control for the error filter: a scripted listing error that is neither an IO nor an
+    /// access error escapes the scripted tree, and a walk with a catch-all filter hides it; the production
+    /// binding requires the walk to let it propagate.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnexpectedListingError_EscapesTheScriptedTree()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"))
+            .Throws("/p/a", () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"));
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => LegacyRecursiveWalk(tree.List, "/p"));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => SwallowsEverythingWalk(tree.List, "/p")));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the scripted cases: <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker"/> over each scripted tree yields
+    /// the required directories and files, and both its directory walk and its file walk list exactly the required
+    /// directories (never a link, never the contents of an unlistable directory).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_ProductionWalk_YieldsTheRequiredOutput(string name)
+    {
+        var (treeFactory, root, directories, files, listed) = Cases[name];
+        var directoryTree = treeFactory();
+        var fileTree = treeFactory();
+
+        var yieldedDirectories = new DirectoryTreeWalker(directoryTree.List).EnumerateDirectories(root).ToList();
+        var yieldedFiles = new DirectoryTreeWalker(fileTree.List).EnumerateFiles(root).ToList();
+
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), yieldedDirectories, yieldedFiles, directoryTree.Listed);
+        AssertWalk((directories, files, listed), yieldedDirectories, yieldedFiles, fileTree.Listed);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the error filter: a listing error that is neither an IO nor an access error
+    /// propagates out of <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker"/> instead of being skipped.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionWalk_UnexpectedListingError_Propagates()
+    {
+        var tree = new ScriptedDirectoryTree()
+            .Lists("/p", DirEntry("/p/a"))
+            .Throws("/p/a", () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"));
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(tree.List).EnumerateDirectories("/p").ToList());
+        Assert.Throws<ArgumentNullException>(() => new DirectoryTreeWalker(null!));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Integration: on the real file system, <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.ListFileSystem"/> flags a directory
+    /// symbolic link and a file symbolic link as links, and <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem"/> neither
+    /// yields nor descends the directory link (whose target lies outside the walked tree) while it yields the file
+    /// link as a file. Creating symbolic links depends on the host (on Windows, Developer Mode or the
+    /// create-symbolic-link privilege), so this is an integration test.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    [Trait("Category", "Integration")]
+    public void FileSystemWalk_RealLinks_AreFlaggedAndNotDescended()
+    {
+        var root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-walk-links-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+        var outside = root + "-outside";
+        var real = Path.Combine(root, "real");
+        var realFile = Path.Combine(real, "x.md");
+        var directoryLink = Path.Combine(root, "dir-link");
+        var fileLink = Path.Combine(root, "file-link.md");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(real);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(outside);
+        System.IO.File.WriteAllText(realFile, "x");
+        System.IO.File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(outside, "y.md"), "y");
+        try
+        {
+            Directory.CreateSymbolicLink(directoryLink, outside);
+            System.IO.File.CreateSymbolicLink(fileLink, realFile);
+
+            var entries = DirectoryTreeWalker.ListFileSystem(root);
+            Assert.Contains(new DirectoryListingEntry(directoryLink, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: true), entries);
+            Assert.Contains(new DirectoryListingEntry(fileLink, IsDirectory: false, IsLink: true), entries);
+            Assert.Contains(new DirectoryListingEntry(real, IsDirectory: true, IsLink: false), entries);
+
+            Assert.Equal(new[] { real }, DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.EnumerateDirectories(root));
+            Assert.Equal(
+                new[] { fileLink, realFile }.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal),
+                DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem.EnumerateFiles(root).Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+        }
+        finally
+        {
+            if (System.IO.File.Exists(fileLink))
+                System.IO.File.Delete(fileLink);
+            if (Directory.Exists(directoryLink))
+                Directory.Delete(directoryLink);
+            Directory.Delete(root, recursive: true);
+            Directory.Delete(outside, recursive: true);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Wrong walks used only as negative controls.
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) LegacyRecursiveWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root)
+    {
+        var directories = new List<string>();
+        var files = new List<string>();
+        var pending = new Queue<string>([root]);
+        while (pending.Count > 0)
+        {
+            foreach (var entry in list(pending.Dequeue()))
+            {
+                if (entry.IsDirectory)
+                {
+                    directories.Add(entry.Path);
+                    pending.Enqueue(entry.Path);
+                }
+                else
+                {
+                    files.Add(entry.Path);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        return (directories, files);
+    }
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) FollowsLinksWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: false, stopAtFirstError: false, yieldUnlistable: true, catchAll: false);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) StopsAtFirstErrorWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: true, stopAtFirstError: true, yieldUnlistable: true, catchAll: false);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) DropsUnlistableDirectoryWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: true, stopAtFirstError: false, yieldUnlistable: false, catchAll: false);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) SwallowsEverythingWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list, string root) =>
+        ConfigurableWalk(list, root, skipLinks: true, stopAtFirstError: false, yieldUnlistable: true, catchAll: true);
+
+    private static (List<string> Directories, List<string> Files) ConfigurableWalk(
+        Func<string, IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>> list,
+        string root,
+        bool skipLinks,
+        bool stopAtFirstError,
+        bool yieldUnlistable,
+        bool catchAll)
+    {
+        var directories = new List<string>();
+        var files = new List<string>();
+        var pending = new Queue<string>([root]);
+        while (pending.Count > 0)
+        {
+            var directory = pending.Dequeue();
+            IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> entries;
+            try
+            {
+                entries = list(directory);
+            }
+            catch (Exception ex) when (catchAll || ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException)
+            {
+                if (!yieldUnlistable)
+                    directories.Remove(directory);
+                if (stopAtFirstError)
+                    break;
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            foreach (var entry in entries)
+            {
+                if (entry.IsDirectory && entry.IsLink && skipLinks)
+                    continue;
+                if (entry.IsDirectory)
+                {
+                    directories.Add(entry.Path);
+                    pending.Enqueue(entry.Path);
+                }
+                else
+                {
+                    files.Add(entry.Path);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        return (directories, files);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted in-memory directory tree: each directory lists fixed entries or throws a fixed exception, every
+    /// listing is recorded in order, and an unscripted directory throws <see cref="InvalidOperationException"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedDirectoryTree
+    {
+        private readonly Dictionary<string, Func<IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry>>> _listings = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Directories listed so far, in order.</summary>
+        public List<string> Listed { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Scripts <paramref name="directory"/> to list <paramref name="entries"/>.</summary>
+        public ScriptedDirectoryTree Lists(string directory, params DirectoryListingEntry[] entries)
+        {
+            _listings[directory] = () => entries;
+            return this;
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>Scripts <paramref name="directory"/> to throw the exception <paramref name="error"/> creates.</summary>
+        public ScriptedDirectoryTree Throws(string directory, Func<Exception> error)
+        {
+            _listings[directory] = () => throw error();
+            return this;
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>Lists one directory as scripted.</summary>
+        public IReadOnlyList<DirectoryListingEntry> List(string directory)
+        {
+            Listed.Add(directory);
+            if (!_listings.TryGetValue(directory, out var listing))
+                throw new InvalidOperationException($"Unscripted directory listed: {directory}");
+            return listing();
+        }
+    }
+}

+    private static bool UsesHostWorkspace(ProcessRunRequest? request, string workspacePath)
+        => request != null
+           && request.FileName == "gh"
+           && request.WorkingDirectory == Path.GetFullPath(workspacePath);

+    private static bool OpensWorkspaceOnHost(ProcessRunRequest? request)
+        => request != null
+           && (request.WorkingDirectory is not null
+               || (request.EnvironmentVariables?.ContainsKey("GIT_CONFIG_COUNT") ?? false));

new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,290 @@
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests how marker agent-plugin version resolution reads one candidate root's version files (<c>.version</c>,
+/// then the plugin manifests and <c>package.json</c>, in that order): a file that exists but cannot be read (an IO
+/// or access error) yields no version, so the read goes on to the next file, and any other error propagates.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 (operator 2026-10-20: "Fold R3-P3 in first", whose
+/// option text adds "a version file that cannot be read never fails the marker" to the criterion with a scripted
+/// test and negative controls, a mock gate, then the fix in MarkerFileService). Test data: scripted version files
+/// (<see cref="ScriptedVersionFiles"/>) under a relative root, whose reads return fixed text, report the file as
+/// absent, or throw the exceptions a locked, denied or vanished file raises; no file system is read. Expected
+/// versions are derived from the criterion and from the reading order the resolution already had: the first file
+/// with a non-empty version wins, an unreadable file counts as one without a version, and malformed JSON still
+/// counts as no version. Negative controls run deliberately wrong reads (the previous read, which let read errors
+/// propagate; one that stops at the first unreadable file; one that hides every error) over the same files and
+/// prove the version assertion rejects each.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class PluginVersionFileReadTests
+{
+    private const string Root = "plugin-root";
+
+    private const string LockedMessage =
+        "The process cannot access the file because it is being used by another process.";
+
+    /// <summary>A version read under test: reads the version files of <c>root</c> through <c>readText</c>.</summary>
+    private delegate string? ReadFunction(string root, Func<string, string?> readText);
+
+    /// <summary>The version files a root may hold, in the order the resolution reads them.</summary>
+    private static readonly string[] VersionFileOrder =
+    [
+        ".version",
+        "plugin.json",
+        Path.Combine(".codex-plugin", "plugin.json"),
+        Path.Combine(".claude-plugin", "plugin.json"),
+        Path.Combine(".grok-plugin", "plugin.json"),
+        "package.json",
+    ];
+
+    private static string FileAt(string relative) => Path.Combine(Root, relative);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted cases: the version files and the version the criterion requires. Each case builds fresh files so
+    /// the read record starts empty.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static readonly Dictionary<string, (Func<ScriptedVersionFiles> Files, string? Version)> Cases =
+        new(StringComparer.Ordinal)
+        {
+            ["version-file"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Holds(FileAt(".version"), "3.1.0\n"),
+                "3.1.0"),
+            ["version-file-locked-manifest-next"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Throws(FileAt(".version"), () => new IOException(LockedMessage))
+                    .Holds(FileAt("plugin.json"), """{"version":"3.2.0"}"""),
+                "3.2.0"),
+            ["version-file-denied-nothing-else"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Throws(FileAt(".version"), () => new UnauthorizedAccessException("Access to the path is denied.")),
+                null),
+            ["manifest-locked-next-manifest"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Throws(FileAt("plugin.json"), () => new IOException(LockedMessage))
+                    .Holds(FileAt(Path.Combine(".claude-plugin", "plugin.json")), """{"version":"3.3.0"}"""),
+                "3.3.0"),
+            ["every-file-unreadable"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Throws(FileAt(".version"), () => new IOException(LockedMessage))
+                    .Throws(FileAt("plugin.json"), () => new UnauthorizedAccessException("Access to the path is denied."))
+                    .Throws(FileAt("package.json"), () => new IOException(LockedMessage)),
+                null),
+            ["version-file-vanished"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Throws(FileAt(".version"), () => new FileNotFoundException("Could not find file.", FileAt(".version")))
+                    .Holds(FileAt("package.json"), """{"version":"3.4.0"}"""),
+                "3.4.0"),
+            ["empty-and-malformed-fall-through"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+                    .Holds(FileAt(".version"), "   ")
+                    .Holds(FileAt("plugin.json"), "not json")
+                    .Holds(FileAt("package.json"), """{"version":"3.5.0"}"""),
+                "3.5.0"),
+            ["no-version-files"] = (
+                () => new ScriptedVersionFiles(),
+                null),
+        };
+
+    /// <summary>The scripted case names, for the theories.</summary>
+    public static TheoryData<string> CaseNames => new(Cases.Keys.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal));
+
+    /// <summary>Asserts one read result against the version a case requires.</summary>
+    private static void AssertVersion(string? expected, string? actual) => Assert.Equal(expected, actual);
+
+    /// <summary>Runs one read over a fresh copy of a case's files and asserts its result.</summary>
+    private static void RunAndAssert(string name, ReadFunction read)
+    {
+        var (filesFactory, version) = Cases[name];
+        var files = filesFactory();
+        AssertVersion(version, read(Root, files.Read));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: each case's required version passes the version assertion.</summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_RequiredVersion_PassesTheVersionAssertion(string name) =>
+        AssertVersion(Cases[name].Version, Cases[name].Version);
+
+    /// <summary>The theories cover every scripted case.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedTheory_CoversEveryCase() => Assert.Equal(8, Cases.Count);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The scripted files are themselves correct: a read records the path, returns the scripted text, throws the
+    /// scripted exception, and reports an unscripted file as absent (null), the way a file that does not exist is
+    /// reported.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedFiles_RecordReadsAndReportUnscriptedFilesAbsent()
+    {
+        var files = new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+            .Holds(FileAt(".version"), "1.0.0")
+            .Throws(FileAt("plugin.json"), () => new IOException(LockedMessage));
+
+        Assert.Equal("1.0.0", files.Read(FileAt(".version")));
+        Assert.Equal(LockedMessage, Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => files.Read(FileAt("plugin.json"))).Message);
+        Assert.Null(files.Read(FileAt("package.json")));
+        Assert.Equal(new[] { FileAt(".version"), FileAt("plugin.json"), FileAt("package.json") }, files.Reads);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: each wrong read is rejected by the version assertion on the case that exposes
+    /// it, inside a passing harness. The previous read (read errors propagate) fails on every case with an
+    /// unreadable file; a read that stops at the first unreadable file loses the readable file after it.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WrongReadNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "version-file-locked-manifest-next", "version-file-denied-nothing-else", "manifest-locked-next-manifest", "every-file-unreadable", "version-file-vanished" })
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert(name, PreviousRead)));
+
+        foreach (var name in new[] { "version-file-locked-manifest-next", "manifest-locked-next-manifest", "version-file-vanished" })
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert(name, StopsAtFirstUnreadableRead)));
+
+        // Each wrong read is wrong only where a case exposes it: on the cases without an unreadable file, every one
+        // of them yields the required version, so the rejections above come from the behavior under test and not
+        // from an assertion that rejects everything.
+        foreach (var read in new ReadFunction[] { PreviousRead, StopsAtFirstUnreadableRead, HidesEveryErrorRead })
+        {
+            foreach (var name in new[] { "version-file", "empty-and-malformed-fall-through", "no-version-files" })
+                Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => RunAndAssert(name, read)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative control for the error filter: a scripted read error that is neither an IO nor an access
+    /// error escapes the scripted files, and a read that hides every error swallows it; the production binding
+    /// requires the read to let it propagate.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void UnexpectedReadError_EscapesTheScriptedFiles()
+    {
+        var files = new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+            .Throws(FileAt(".version"), () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"))
+            .Holds(FileAt("package.json"), """{"version":"3.6.0"}""");
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => PreviousRead(Root, files.Read));
+        Assert.Equal("3.6.0", HidesEveryErrorRead(Root, files.Read));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the scripted cases: <see cref="MarkerFileService.TryReadAgentPluginVersion"/> over each
+    /// case's files yields the required version through the same assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="name">Scripted case name.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [MemberData(nameof(CaseNames))]
+    public void ScriptedCase_ProductionRead_YieldsTheRequiredVersion(string name) =>
+        RunAndAssert(name, MarkerFileService.TryReadAgentPluginVersion);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Production binding of the error filter: a read error that is neither an IO nor an access error propagates out
+    /// of <see cref="MarkerFileService.TryReadAgentPluginVersion"/> instead of being treated as a missing version.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ProductionRead_UnexpectedReadError_Propagates()
+    {
+        var files = new ScriptedVersionFiles()
+            .Throws(FileAt(".version"), () => new InvalidOperationException("not an IO error"))
+            .Holds(FileAt("package.json"), """{"version":"3.6.0"}""");
+
+        Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => MarkerFileService.TryReadAgentPluginVersion(Root, files.Read));
+    }
+
+    // Wrong reads used only as negative controls.
+
+    private static string? PreviousRead(string root, Func<string, string?> readText) =>
+        ConfigurableRead(root, readText, tolerateReadErrors: false, stopAtFirstUnreadable: false, hideEveryError: false);
+
+    private static string? StopsAtFirstUnreadableRead(string root, Func<string, string?> readText) =>
+        ConfigurableRead(root, readText, tolerateReadErrors: true, stopAtFirstUnreadable: true, hideEveryError: false);
+
+    private static string? HidesEveryErrorRead(string root, Func<string, string?> readText) =>
+        ConfigurableRead(root, readText, tolerateReadErrors: true, stopAtFirstUnreadable: false, hideEveryError: true);
+
+    private static string? ConfigurableRead(
+        string root,
+        Func<string, string?> readText,
+        bool tolerateReadErrors,
+        bool stopAtFirstUnreadable,
+        bool hideEveryError)
+    {
+        foreach (var relative in VersionFileOrder)
+        {
+            string? text;
+            try
+            {
+                text = readText(Path.Combine(root, relative));
+            }
+            catch (Exception ex) when (hideEveryError || (tolerateReadErrors && ex is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException))
+            {
+                if (stopAtFirstUnreadable)
+                    return null;
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            if (text is null)
+                continue;
+            var version = relative == ".version" ? text.Trim() : VersionFromJson(text);
+            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(version))
+                return version;
+        }
+
+        return null;
+    }
+
+    private static string? VersionFromJson(string text)
+    {
+        try
+        {
+            using var document = System.Text.Json.JsonDocument.Parse(text);
+            return document.RootElement.TryGetProperty("version", out var version)
+                && version.ValueKind == System.Text.Json.JsonValueKind.String
+                ? version.GetString()
+                : null;
+        }
+        catch (System.Text.Json.JsonException)
+        {
+            return null;
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Scripted version files: each path returns fixed text or throws a fixed exception, every read is recorded in
+    /// order, and an unscripted path reads as absent (null).
+    /// </summary>
+    private sealed class ScriptedVersionFiles
+    {
+        private readonly Dictionary<string, Func<string>> _files = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);
+
+        /// <summary>Paths read so far, in order.</summary>
+        public List<string> Reads { get; } = [];
+
+        /// <summary>Scripts <paramref name="path"/> to hold <paramref name="text"/>.</summary>
+        public ScriptedVersionFiles Holds(string path, string text)
+        {
+            _files[path] = () => text;
+            return this;
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>Scripts <paramref name="path"/> to throw the exception <paramref name="error"/> creates.</summary>
+        public ScriptedVersionFiles Throws(string path, Func<Exception> error)
+        {
+            _files[path] = () => throw error();
+            return this;
+        }
+
+        /// <summary>Reads one file as scripted; null when the path is not scripted (the file does not exist).</summary>
+        public string? Read(string path)
+        {
+            Reads.Add(path);
+            return _files.TryGetValue(path, out var read) ? read() : null;
+        }
+    }
+}

+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;
+using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Storage.Entities;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Production binding of the directory-walk cases to the two user-profile scans: marker agent-plugin version
+/// resolution and the session-log agent-history scan each keep working when a directory under the profile is an
+/// untrusted mount point (its listing throws) or a link, and neither scan fails.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 and TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 /
+/// AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009 (operator 2026-10-20: "Fix scans in PR 87 (Recommended)"; triage report
+/// b0e41c36). Test data: a temporary profile the test creates, listed through
+/// <see cref="DirectoryTreeWalker.ListFileSystem"/>, with the failure conditions scripted on top: one directory's
+/// listing throws the untrusted-mount-point <see cref="IOException"/> seen on PAYTON-LEGION2 as kingd, and one
+/// directory is reported as a link. Seam controls run the same profile with the link flag removed and prove the
+/// result changes, so the expected values come from the link and error handling rather than from the layout. The
+/// version-file cases (operator 2026-10-20: "Fold R3-P3 in first") read the same profile through
+/// <see cref="MarkerFileService.ReadExistingFileText"/> with named <c>.version</c> files scripted to throw the
+/// sharing-violation <see cref="IOException"/> of a locked file; their seam control reads every file normally.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class UserProfileScanBindingTests : IDisposable
+{
+    private const string UntrustedMountPointMessage =
+        "The path cannot be traversed because it contains an untrusted mount point.";
+
+    private const string PluginName = "mcpserver-scan-binding-plugin";
+
+    private readonly string _root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"mcp-scan-binding-{Guid.NewGuid():N}");
+
+    /// <summary>Creates the temporary root that holds the profile and the workspace.</summary>
+    public UserProfileScanBindingTests() => Directory.CreateDirectory(_root);
+
+    /// <summary>Deletes the temporary root.</summary>
+    public void Dispose()
+    {
+        if (Directory.Exists(_root))
+            Directory.Delete(_root, recursive: true);
+    }
+
+    private string Profile => Path.Combine(_root, "profile");
+
+    private string Workspace => Path.Combine(_root, "workspace");
+
+    private static bool SamePath(string left, string right) =>
+        string.Equals(Path.GetFullPath(left), Path.GetFullPath(right), StringComparison.Ordinal);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// A walker over the real temporary profile in which <paramref name="unlistable"/> throws the untrusted-mount-point
+    /// error and <paramref name="link"/> (when given) is reported as a directory link.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static DirectoryTreeWalker ScriptedWalker(string unlistable, string? link) =>
+        new(directory =>
+        {
+            if (SamePath(directory, unlistable))
+                throw new IOException(UntrustedMountPointMessage);
+            return DirectoryTreeWalker.ListFileSystem(directory)
+                .Select(entry => link is not null && SamePath(entry.Path, link) ? entry with { IsLink = true } : entry)
+                .ToList();
+        });
+
+    private (string Unlistable, string Linked) CreatePluginCacheProfile()
+    {
+        var cache = Path.Combine(Profile, ".claude", "plugins", "cache");
+        var reachable = Path.Combine(cache, PluginName, "7.7.7");
+        var linked = Path.Combine(cache, PluginName, "9.9.9");
+        var unlistable = Path.Combine(cache, "caveman");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(reachable);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(linked);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(unlistable, "compress", "scripts"));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Workspace);
+        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(reachable, ".version"), "7.7.7");
+        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(linked, ".version"), "9.9.9");
+        return (unlistable, linked);
+    }
+
+    private string Resolve(DirectoryTreeWalker walker) =>
+        MarkerFileService.ResolveAgentPluginVersion(
+            Workspace,
+            PluginName,
+            $"MCP_SCAN_BINDING_UNSET_{Guid.NewGuid():N}",
+            Profile,
+            walker);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001: with an untrusted mount point and a linked higher version in the Claude plugin cache,
+    /// resolution does not throw and returns the reachable version; with the link flag removed (seam control) the
+    /// higher version wins, so the link is what was skipped.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ResolveAgentPluginVersion_UnlistableAndLinkedCacheDirectories_ResolveTheReachableVersion()
+    {
+        var (unlistable, linked) = CreatePluginCacheProfile();
+
+        Assert.Equal("7.7.7", Resolve(ScriptedWalker(unlistable, linked)));
+        Assert.Equal("9.9.9", Resolve(ScriptedWalker(unlistable, link: null)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001: when the plugin cache root itself cannot be listed, resolution does not throw and
+    /// falls back to the synced plugin version.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ResolveAgentPluginVersion_CacheRootUnlistable_FallsBackToTheSyncedVersion()
+    {
+        CreatePluginCacheProfile();
+        var cacheRoot = Path.Combine(Profile, ".claude", "plugins", "cache");
+
+        Assert.Equal(MarkerFileService.SyncedAgentPluginVersion, Resolve(ScriptedWalker(cacheRoot, link: null)));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The file system read, except that every path in <paramref name="unreadable"/> throws the sharing-violation
+    /// <see cref="IOException"/> a locked file raises.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static Func<string, string?> ScriptedReader(params string[] unreadable) =>
+        path => unreadable.Any(locked => SamePath(path, locked))
+            ? throw new IOException("The process cannot access the file because it is being used by another process.")
+            : MarkerFileService.ReadExistingFileText(path);
+
+    private string ResolveWithReader(Func<string, string?> readText) =>
+        MarkerFileService.ResolveAgentPluginVersion(
+            Workspace,
+            PluginName,
+            $"MCP_SCAN_BINDING_UNSET_{Guid.NewGuid():N}",
+            Profile,
+            DirectoryTreeWalker.FileSystem,
+            readText);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001: when the higher cached version's <c>.version</c> cannot be read, resolution does not
+    /// throw and returns the next readable version; with the plain file read (seam control) the higher version wins,
+    /// so the unreadable file is what was passed over.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ResolveAgentPluginVersion_UnreadableVersionFile_ResolvesTheNextReadableVersion()
+    {
+        var (_, higher) = CreatePluginCacheProfile();
+
+        Assert.Equal("7.7.7", ResolveWithReader(ScriptedReader(Path.Combine(higher, ".version"))));
+        Assert.Equal("9.9.9", ResolveWithReader(MarkerFileService.ReadExistingFileText));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001: when every cached version file cannot be read, resolution does not throw and falls back
+    /// to the synced plugin version.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ResolveAgentPluginVersion_EveryVersionFileUnreadable_FallsBackToTheSyncedVersion()
+    {
+        var (_, higher) = CreatePluginCacheProfile();
+        var lower = Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(higher)!, "7.7.7");
+
+        Assert.Equal(
+            MarkerFileService.SyncedAgentPluginVersion,
+            ResolveWithReader(ScriptedReader(Path.Combine(higher, ".version"), Path.Combine(lower, ".version"))));
+    }
+
+    private (string Unlistable, string Linked) CreateHistoryProfile()
+    {
+        var claude = Path.Combine(Profile, ".claude");
+        var history = Path.Combine(claude, "projects", "agent-scanbinding");
+        var unlistable = Path.Combine(claude, "plugins", "marketplaces", "caveman");
+        var linked = Path.Combine(claude, "plugins", "cache", "linked");
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(history);
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(unlistable, "compress", "scripts"));
+        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(linked, "agent-scanbinding"));
+        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(history, "chat.jsonl"), "working PLAN-SCANBINDING-001");
+        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(linked, "agent-scanbinding", "chat.jsonl"), "working PLAN-LINKEDCOPY-001");
+        return (unlistable, linked);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009: with an untrusted mount point and a linked history folder under ~/.claude, the
+    /// extractor does not throw and takes the TODO id from the reachable history only; with the link flag removed
+    /// (seam control) the linked copy is read too and the tied ids yield None.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Extract_UnlistableAndLinkedHistoryDirectories_ReadTheReachableHistory()
+    {
+        var (unlistable, linked) = CreateHistoryProfile();
+
+        var skipped = new SessionLogTurnContextExtractor(ScriptedWalker(unlistable, linked))
+            .Extract(new SessionLogTurnEntity(), workspacePath: null, userProfilePath: Profile, agentSessionId: "agent-scanbinding");
+        var followed = new SessionLogTurnContextExtractor(ScriptedWalker(unlistable, link: null))
+            .Extract(new SessionLogTurnEntity(), workspacePath: null, userProfilePath: Profile, agentSessionId: "agent-scanbinding");
+
+        Assert.Equal("PLAN-SCANBINDING-001", skipped.TodoId);
+        Assert.Equal(SessionLogTurnContextValidator.NoneSentinel, followed.TodoId);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// AC-TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006-009: when ~/.claude itself cannot be listed, the extractor does not throw and the
+    /// turn keeps None.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Extract_HistoryRootUnlistable_KeepsNone()
+    {
+        CreateHistoryProfile();
+
+        var result = new SessionLogTurnContextExtractor(ScriptedWalker(Path.Combine(Profile, ".claude"), link: null))
+            .Extract(new SessionLogTurnEntity(), workspacePath: null, userProfilePath: Profile, agentSessionId: "agent-scanbinding");
+
+        Assert.Equal(SessionLogTurnContextValidator.NoneSentinel, result.TodoId);
+        Assert.Equal(SessionLogTurnContextValidator.NoneSentinel, result.PlanFile);
+    }
+}

+/// Tests that the pg_ctl calls of <see cref="EphemeralPostgresFixture"/> are silent (<c>-s</c>) and wait (<c>-w</c>).
+/// pg_ctl runs without output redirection, so it writes to the test process's standard output, which carries the
+/// xUnit v3 test framework's JSON messages to the VSTest adapter; its informational lines ("waiting for server to
+/// start.... done", "server started") garbled those messages and lost test results in the Windows integration lane.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2 (every integration case runs and passes in its host's lane at
+/// 0 failed and 0 not executed). Found by the diagnostic lane ug-r3-win-lane-diag-2, whose testhost log shows
+/// "Received unparseable output from test process: waiting for server to start....{"$type":"test-passed",...}";
+/// fix chosen by the operator on 2026-10-20 ("Diagnose the loss in PR 87"). Test data: scripted pg_ctl argument
+/// strings; the start and stop strings the fixture used before are the negative controls. No process is started.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class EphemeralPostgresFixtureArgumentsTests
+{
+    private static readonly Regex Silent = new(@"(^|\s)-s(\s|$)", RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
+
+    private static readonly Regex Wait = new(@"(^|\s)-w(\s|$)", RegexOptions.CultureInvariant);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that a pg_ctl argument string runs the given action (<c>start</c> or <c>stop</c>) silently and waits
+    /// for it to complete.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertSilentWaitingCall(string arguments, string action)
+    {
+        Assert.StartsWith(action + " ", arguments, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.Matches(Silent, arguments);
+        Assert.Matches(Wait, arguments);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: silent, waiting start and stop strings pass the assertion.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ScriptedSilentCalls_PassTheAssertion()
+    {
+        AssertSilentWaitingCall("start -D \"C:\\data\" -w -s -o \"-p 5432 -c listen_addresses=localhost\" -l \"C:\\data\\pg.log\"", "start");
+        AssertSilentWaitingCall("stop -D \"C:\\data\" -m immediate -w -s", "stop");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: the start and stop strings the fixture used before (no <c>-s</c>), a silent call
+    /// that does not wait, a silent stop asserted as a start, and an <c>-s</c> that only appears inside the quoted
+    /// server options are each rejected, inside a passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void LoudOrNonWaitingCalls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertSilentWaitingCall("start -D \"C:\\data\" -w -o \"-p 5432 -c listen_addresses=localhost\" -l \"C:\\data\\pg.log\"", "start")));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertSilentWaitingCall("stop -D \"C:\\data\" -m immediate -w", "stop")));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertSilentWaitingCall("stop -D \"C:\\data\" -m immediate -s", "stop")));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertSilentWaitingCall("stop -D \"C:\\data\" -m immediate -w -s", "start")));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertSilentWaitingCall("start -D \"C:\\data\" -w -o \"-p 5432 -s\" -l \"C:\\data\\pg.log\"", "start")));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: the fixture's own start and stop argument builders pass the same assertion, and the start arguments
+    /// keep the port, the localhost listener and the server log file.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void FixtureCalls_AreSilentAndWait()
+    {
+        var start = EphemeralPostgresFixture.PgCtlStartArguments(@"C:\data", 5432, @"C:\data\pg.log");
+        AssertSilentWaitingCall(start, "start");
+        Assert.Contains("-o \"-p 5432 -c listen_addresses=localhost\"", start, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        Assert.Contains("-l \"C:\\data\\pg.log\"", start, StringComparison.Ordinal);
+        AssertSilentWaitingCall(EphemeralPostgresFixture.PgCtlStopArguments(@"C:\data"), "stop");
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Storage/MigrationKeyAssertions.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Storage/MigrationKeyAssertions.cs

+using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Storage;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-DB-001: raw-SQL workspace-key assertions for SQLite data-migration tests. A data migration copies the parent
+/// workspace key into every backfilled child row (the child foreign key to Workspaces). EF query parameters compared
+/// with workspace keys are canonicalized by WorkspaceKeyConversion, so a legacy key (for example a backslash Windows
+/// spelling) is only observable through SQL that bypasses EF.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class MigrationKeyAssertions
+{
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that the only non-global workspace row has exactly <paramref name="expectedKey"/> as its WorkspaceId,
+    /// and that <paramref name="childTable"/> has rows whose WorkspaceId values are all exactly that key.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="connection">Open connection to the migrated database.</param>
+    /// <param name="childTable">Backfilled child table that carries a WorkspaceId column.</param>
+    /// <param name="expectedKey">The workspace key seeded before the migration, compared ordinally.</param>
+    internal static void AssertChildRowsCarryWorkspaceKey(SqliteConnection connection, string childTable, string expectedKey)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(connection);
+        ArgumentException.ThrowIfNullOrWhiteSpace(childTable);
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(expectedKey);
+
+        var parentKeys = ReadStrings(connection, "SELECT \"WorkspaceId\" FROM \"Workspaces\" WHERE \"WorkspaceId\" <> '' ORDER BY 1;");
+        Assert.Equal([expectedKey], parentKeys);
+
+        var childKeys = ReadStrings(connection, $"SELECT DISTINCT \"WorkspaceId\" FROM \"{childTable}\" ORDER BY 1;");
+        Assert.Equal([expectedKey], childKeys);
+    }
+
+    private static List<string> ReadStrings(SqliteConnection connection, string sql)
+    {
+        using var command = connection.CreateCommand();
+        command.CommandText = sql;
+        using var reader = command.ExecuteReader();
+        var values = new List<string>();
+        while (reader.Read())
+        {
+            values.Add(reader.GetString(0));
+        }
+
+        return values;
+    }
+}

+using Microsoft.Data.Sqlite;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Storage;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-DB-001, BDPv4 mock gate for <see cref="MigrationKeyAssertions"/>: scripted SQLite tables (no migrations)
+/// stand in for a migrated database. The assertion accepts child rows that carry exactly the parent workspace key and
+/// rejects the canonical respelling, mixed keys, an empty child table, a rekeyed parent, and a second workspace, before
+/// the assertion is bound to Decompose4nfBackfillMigrationTests.
+/// </summary>
+public sealed class MigrationKeyAssertionsMockGateTests : IDisposable
+{
+    private const string LegacyKey = "Q:\\__mcp_unit_test__\\QBrainAi";
+    private const string CanonicalKey = "Q:/__mcp_unit_test__/QBrainAi";
+    private readonly SqliteConnection _connection;
+
+    /// <summary>Creates an in-memory database with a Workspaces table (global row seeded) and one child table.</summary>
+    public MigrationKeyAssertionsMockGateTests()
+    {
+        _connection = new SqliteConnection("Data Source=:memory:");
+        _connection.Open();
+        Execute("CREATE TABLE \"Workspaces\" (\"WorkspaceId\" TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY);");
+        Execute("CREATE TABLE \"ChildItems\" (\"Id\" INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, \"WorkspaceId\" TEXT NOT NULL, \"Value\" TEXT NOT NULL);");
+        Execute("INSERT INTO \"Workspaces\" VALUES ('');");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>TR-MCP-DB-001: child rows that carry exactly the seeded legacy parent key are accepted.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void ChildRowsWithParentKey_AreAccepted()
+    {
+        SeedParent(LegacyKey);
+        SeedChild(LegacyKey, "GPL-3.0");
+        SeedChild(LegacyKey, "AGPL-3.0");
+
+        MigrationKeyAssertions.AssertChildRowsCarryWorkspaceKey(_connection, "ChildItems", LegacyKey);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-DB-001 negative controls: each wrong database state is rejected by the assertion (canonical respelling in
+    /// the child, mixed keys, no child rows, parent rekeyed while the child is not, a second workspace).
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="fault">The injected wrong state.</param>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("child-canonical")]
+    [InlineData("child-mixed")]
+    [InlineData("child-empty")]
+    [InlineData("parent-canonical")]
+    [InlineData("second-workspace")]
+    public void WrongKeyState_IsRejected(string fault)
+    {
+        switch (fault)
+        {
+            case "child-canonical":
+                SeedParent(LegacyKey);
+                SeedChild(CanonicalKey, "GPL-3.0");
+                break;
+            case "child-mixed":
+                SeedParent(LegacyKey);
+                SeedChild(LegacyKey, "GPL-3.0");
+                SeedChild(CanonicalKey, "AGPL-3.0");
+                break;
+            case "child-empty":
+                SeedParent(LegacyKey);
+                break;
+            case "parent-canonical":
+                SeedParent(CanonicalKey);
+                SeedChild(LegacyKey, "GPL-3.0");
+                break;
+            case "second-workspace":
+                SeedParent(LegacyKey);
+                SeedParent("Q:\\other");
+                SeedChild(LegacyKey, "GPL-3.0");
+                break;
+            default:
+                throw new InvalidOperationException("Unknown fault " + fault);
+        }
+
+        Assert.ThrowsAny<Xunit.Sdk.XunitException>(
+            () => MigrationKeyAssertions.AssertChildRowsCarryWorkspaceKey(_connection, "ChildItems", LegacyKey));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Releases the in-memory database connection.</summary>
+    public void Dispose()
+    {
+        _connection.Dispose();
+    }
+
+    private void SeedParent(string key) => Execute("INSERT INTO \"Workspaces\" VALUES ($key);", key);
+
+    private void SeedChild(string key, string value)
+    {
+        using var command = _connection.CreateCommand();
+        command.CommandText = "INSERT INTO \"ChildItems\" (\"WorkspaceId\", \"Value\") VALUES ($key, $value);";
+        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("$key", key);
+        command.Parameters.AddWithValue("$value", value);
+        command.ExecuteNonQuery();
+    }
+
+    private void Execute(string sql, string? key = null)
+    {
+        using var command = _connection.CreateCommand();
+        command.CommandText = sql;
+        if (key is not null)
+        {
+            command.Parameters.AddWithValue("$key", key);
+        }
+
+        command.ExecuteNonQuery();
+    }
+}

+using System.Reflection;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Storage;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests that the test classes which open connections to the shared LocalDB instance belong to one xUnit collection,
+/// so they run one after another instead of creating and migrating databases on the same instance at once.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2 (every integration case runs and passes in its host's lane at
+/// 0 failed). Found by the Windows interactive lane ug-r3-win-lane-4, where a migration command of
+/// SqlServerAuditPayloadMigrationTests waited past its 120 s command timeout while several SQL Server test classes
+/// migrated fresh databases on one LocalDB instance; fix chosen by the operator on 2026-10-20 ("Serialize SQL Server
+/// tests (Recommended)"), whose option text says the classes "join one xUnit collection with parallelization
+/// disabled, so they run one at a time against LocalDB". Test data: scripted nested types carrying a collection
+/// attribute with the required name, another name, or none, and scripted collection definitions with the flag set,
+/// absent or false (each under its own name, so no definition duplicates the real one, and no class is a member); the
+/// attributes are read through <see cref="CustomAttributeData"/>, so no test is run and no server is contacted.
+/// Negative controls prove the membership assertion rejects a type outside any collection and a type in another
+/// collection, and the definition assertion rejects a definition without the flag, one with the flag false, a wrong
+/// name and a missing definition. The binding then checks the real definition and the four classes that open LocalDB
+/// connections; a new class that opens LocalDB must be added to the list (stated limit: the list is not discovered
+/// from source).
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class SqlServerLocalDbCollectionTests
+{
+    private const string RequiredCollection = "SQL Server LocalDB";
+
+    private const string ScriptedSerializedName = "Scripted serialized collection";
+
+    private const string ScriptedParallelName = "Scripted parallel collection";
+
+    private const string ScriptedExplicitParallelName = "Scripted explicit parallel collection";
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="type"/> carries exactly one xUnit collection attribute and that it names
+    /// <paramref name="collection"/>.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertInCollection(Type type, string collection)
+    {
+        var names = type.GetCustomAttributesData()
+            .Where(attribute => attribute.AttributeType == typeof(CollectionAttribute))
+            .Select(attribute => attribute.ConstructorArguments.Count == 1 ? attribute.ConstructorArguments[0].Value as string : null)
+            .ToList();
+        var name = Assert.Single(names);
+        Assert.Equal(collection, name);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="definition"/> carries exactly one xUnit collection definition, that it names
+    /// <paramref name="collection"/>, and that it sets <c>DisableParallelization</c> to true, so the collection never
+    /// runs beside another collection.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertSerializedDefinition(Type definition, string collection)
+    {
+        var attribute = Assert.Single(
+            definition.GetCustomAttributesData(),
+            data => data.AttributeType == typeof(CollectionDefinitionAttribute));
+        var name = Assert.Single(attribute.ConstructorArguments);
+        Assert.Equal(collection, name.Value as string);
+        var flag = Assert.Single(
+            attribute.NamedArguments,

+using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Storage;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2: the server connection of the SQL Server integration test classes. The built-in default is
+/// LocalDB with a 120 s connect timeout (matching the classes' 120 s command timeout), so a LocalDB instance that is
+/// still starting while several SQL Server test classes open it at once does not fail the Windows integration lane
+/// (ug-r3-win-lane-2 failed after SqlClient's 15 s default). <c>MCP_TEST_SQLSERVER_CONNECTION</c> names another
+/// server and is used unchanged.
+/// </summary>
+internal static class SqlServerTestConnection
+{
+    /// <summary>Environment variable that names another SQL Server; its value is used unchanged.</summary>
+    internal const string OverrideVariable = "MCP_TEST_SQLSERVER_CONNECTION";
+
+    /// <summary>Seconds a connection may wait for LocalDB to accept it.</summary>
+    internal const int ConnectTimeoutSeconds = 120;
+
+    /// <summary>The built-in LocalDB default: integrated security, a trusted certificate, no database.</summary>
+    internal static string LocalDbDefault { get; } = new SqlConnectionStringBuilder
+    {
+        DataSource = @"(localdb)\MSSQLLocalDB",
+        IntegratedSecurity = true,
+        TrustServerCertificate = true,
+        ConnectTimeout = ConnectTimeoutSeconds,
+    }.ConnectionString;
+
+    /// <summary>The server connection for this process: the override when it is set, otherwise the LocalDB default.</summary>
+    internal static string Server => Resolve(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(OverrideVariable));
+
+    /// <summary>Returns <paramref name="overrideValue"/> when it is set, otherwise <see cref="LocalDbDefault"/>.</summary>
+    /// <param name="overrideValue">The value of <see cref="OverrideVariable"/>, or null when it is unset.</param>
+    /// <returns>The server connection string.</returns>
+    internal static string Resolve(string? overrideValue) => overrideValue ?? LocalDbDefault;
+}

+using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;
+using Xunit;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Storage;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// Tests the server connection that the SQL Server integration test classes use by default: LocalDB with integrated
+/// security, a trusted server certificate, no database, and a 120 s connect timeout, so a LocalDB instance that is
+/// still starting when several SQL Server test classes open it at once does not fail the Windows integration lane.
+/// </summary>
+/// <remarks>
+/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-NUKE-019 AC-2 (every integration case runs and passes in its host's lane at
+/// 0 failed). Found by the Windows interactive lane ug-r3-win-lane-2, where
+/// SqlServerAuditPayloadMigrationTests failed after the 15 s default connect timeout; fix chosen by the operator on
+/// 2026-10-20 ("Fix the flake in PR 87 (Recommended)"). Test data: scripted connection strings parsed with
+/// <see cref="SqlConnectionStringBuilder"/>; no server is contacted and no environment variable is read. Negative
+/// controls feed the default assertion the literal the three classes used before (15 s default), a short timeout,
+/// another server, a string without the trusted certificate, and one that names a database, and prove it rejects each.
+/// </remarks>
+public sealed class SqlServerTestConnectionTests
+{
+    private const string RequiredDefault =
+        "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Integrated Security=True;TrustServerCertificate=True;Connect Timeout=120";
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Asserts that <paramref name="connectionString"/> is the required default: LocalDB, integrated security, a
+    /// trusted server certificate, no database, and a 120 s connect timeout.
+    /// </summary>
+    private static void AssertLocalDbDefault(string connectionString)
+    {
+        var builder = new SqlConnectionStringBuilder(connectionString);
+        Assert.Equal(@"(localdb)\MSSQLLocalDB", builder.DataSource);
+        Assert.True(builder.IntegratedSecurity);
+        Assert.True(builder.TrustServerCertificate);
+        Assert.Equal(string.Empty, builder.InitialCatalog);
+        Assert.Equal(120, builder.ConnectTimeout);
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Mock gate positive control: the required default passes the assertion.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void RequiredDefault_PassesTheDefaultAssertion() => AssertLocalDbDefault(RequiredDefault);
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Mock gate negative controls: the literal the three classes used before (SqlClient's 15 s default connect
+    /// timeout), an 8 s timeout, another server, no trusted certificate, and a named database are each rejected.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void DefaultNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        foreach (var wrong in new[]
+                 {
+                     "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Integrated Security=True;TrustServerCertificate=True",
+                     "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Integrated Security=True;TrustServerCertificate=True;Connect Timeout=8",
+                     "Server=localhost;Integrated Security=True;TrustServerCertificate=True;Connect Timeout=120",
+                     "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Integrated Security=True;Connect Timeout=120",
+                     "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;Database=mcp;Integrated Security=True;TrustServerCertificate=True;Connect Timeout=120",
+                 })
+        {
+            Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertLocalDbDefault(wrong)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// Binding: <see cref="SqlServerTestConnection.LocalDbDefault"/>, and the resolver with no override, pass the
+    /// same default assertion.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void LocalDbDefault_PassesTheDefaultAssertion()
+    {
+        AssertLocalDbDefault(SqlServerTestConnection.LocalDbDefault);
+        AssertLocalDbDefault(SqlServerTestConnection.Resolve(overrideValue: null));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Binding: an override is returned unchanged, with no timeout or flag added.</summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void Resolve_Override_IsReturnedUnchanged()
+    {
+        const string external = "Server=sql.lab;Database=master;User Id=tester;Encrypt=False";
+        Assert.Equal(external, SqlServerTestConnection.Resolve(external));
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Storage/WorkspaceNativePathHostContractTests.cs b/tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests/Storage/WorkspaceNativePathHostContractTests.cs

+using QBrainAi.Client;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Storage;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TEST-MCP-PATHNORM-001 (criterion unitgreen-native-host) / TR-MCP-PATHNORM-001 (plan P1.4c): the
+/// native boundary decides by the injected host, so both its Windows-host and its non-Windows-host
+/// behavior are proven on every test host against a simulated host, without a host-conditional skip
+/// (TR-MCP-USECASE-018 AC-006: no unit test depends on the platform). BDP v4: MockData then Real.
+/// </summary>
+public abstract class WorkspaceNativePathHostContractTests
+{
+    /// <summary>The host-aware native-spelling function under test.</summary>
+    protected abstract string ToNativePath(string path, WorkspacePathPlatform platform, IWorkspaceHostEnvironment host);
+
+    /// <summary>On a Windows host, Windows-platform paths switch to backslashes.</summary>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("C:/Users/kingd/repo", @"C:\Users\kingd\repo")]
+    [InlineData("C:/", @"C:\")]
+    [InlineData("//srv/share/x", @"\\srv\share\x")]
+    [InlineData(@"C:\already\native", @"C:\already\native")]
+    public void WindowsHost_WindowsPath_UsesBackslashes(string path, string expected)
+    {
+        AssertNative(expected, ToNativePath(path, WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows, SimulatedWorkspaceHosts.Windows()));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>On a non-Windows host nothing is rewritten, whatever the platform tag.</summary>
+    [Theory]
+    [InlineData("C:/Users/kingd/repo", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)]
+    [InlineData("//srv/share/x", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)]
+    [InlineData("/home/x/repo", WorkspacePathPlatform.CaseSensitive)]
+    public void NonWindowsHost_IsUnchanged(string path, WorkspacePathPlatform platform)
+    {
+        AssertUnchanged(path, ToNativePath(path, platform, SimulatedWorkspaceHosts.Linux()));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>Assertion shared by the non-Windows-host contract and its negative controls.</summary>
+    /// <param name="path">Path given to the native-spelling function.</param>
+    /// <param name="actual">Value the function returned.</param>
+    protected static void AssertUnchanged(string path, string actual) => Assert.Equal(path, actual);
+
+    /// <summary>Assertion shared by the Windows-host contract and its negative controls.</summary>
+    /// <param name="expected">Backslash spelling the requirement defines.</param>
+    /// <param name="actual">Value the function returned.</param>
+    protected static void AssertNative(string expected, string actual) => Assert.Equal(expected, actual);
+}
+
+/// <summary>BDP v4 step 2: the host-aware native-boundary contract against a literal data table.</summary>
+public sealed class WorkspaceNativePathHostMockDataTests : WorkspaceNativePathHostContractTests
+{
+    private static readonly Dictionary<(string, WorkspacePathPlatform), string> WindowsHost = new()
+    {
+        [("C:/Users/kingd/repo", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)] = @"C:\Users\kingd\repo",
+        [("C:/", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)] = @"C:\",
+        [("//srv/share/x", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)] = @"\\srv\share\x",
+        [(@"C:\already\native", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)] = @"C:\already\native",
+        [("/home/x/repo", WorkspacePathPlatform.CaseSensitive)] = "/home/x/repo",
+    };
+
+    /// <inheritdoc />
+    protected override string ToNativePath(string path, WorkspacePathPlatform platform, IWorkspaceHostEnvironment host)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(host);
+        if (host.Platform != WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows)
+            return path;
+
+        return WindowsHost.TryGetValue((path, platform), out var native)
+            ? native
+            : throw new InvalidOperationException($"No scripted native spelling for '{path}' ({platform}).");
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// BDP v4 step 3 negative controls: a native-spelling function that rewrites on a non-Windows host,
+    /// or that decides by a Windows host instead of the injected one, is rejected by the shared
+    /// assertion inside this passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void NonWindowsHostNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertUnchanged("C:/Users/kingd/repo", @"C:\Users\kingd\repo")));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertUnchanged(
+            "//srv/share/x",
+            ToNativePath("//srv/share/x", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows, SimulatedWorkspaceHosts.Windows()))));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertUnchanged(
+            "/home/x/repo",
+            ToNativePath("/home/x/repo", WorkspacePathPlatform.CaseSensitive, SimulatedWorkspaceHosts.Windows()))));
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006) negative controls: a native-spelling function that keeps forward slashes
+    /// on a Windows host, or that decides by a non-Windows host instead of the injected Windows one, is
+    /// rejected by the shared Windows-host assertion inside this passing harness.
+    /// </summary>
+    [Fact]
+    public void WindowsHostNegativeControls_AreRejectedInsidePassingHarness()
+    {
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertNative(@"C:\Users\kingd\repo", "C:/Users/kingd/repo")));
+        Assert.NotNull(Record.Exception(() => AssertNative(
+            @"\\srv\share\x",
+            ToNativePath("//srv/share/x", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows, SimulatedWorkspaceHosts.Linux()))));
+        Assert.Null(Record.Exception(() => AssertNative(
+            @"C:\",
+            ToNativePath("C:/", WorkspacePathPlatform.Windows, SimulatedWorkspaceHosts.Windows()))));
+    }
+}

diff --git a/tests/Shared/TestRepositoryRoot.cs b/tests/Shared/TestRepositoryRoot.cs
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..c4fa2e20
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/Shared/TestRepositoryRoot.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,124 @@
+using System.Reflection;
+
+namespace QBrainAi.Tests.Shared;
+
+/// <summary>
+/// TR-MCP-USECASE-018 (AC-006), TR-MCP-USECASE-019 (AC-008): the one repository-root locator for the unit-test
+/// projects, linked into each project that reads the checkout, so every test that reads repository files reaches
+/// it. Reading the checkout is an environment dependence (operator 2026-10-19: "If it depends on the environment,
+/// it's an integration test."), so the locator resolves only inside a test case, or a test-class fixture, tagged
+/// <c>[Trait("Category", "Integration")]</c>; a unit test that reaches it fails with the reason, which makes the
+/// full unit run the proof that no unit test reads the repository. The root comes from <c>MCP_REPOSITORY_ROOT</c>,
+/// then the <c>McpRepositoryRoot</c> build metadata, then the first directory above the test assembly, the test
+/// output or the current directory that holds the repository markers (<c>.git</c>, <c>QBrainAi.sln</c>,
+/// <c>Directory.Build.props</c>).
+/// </summary>
+internal static class TestRepositoryRoot
+{
+    private const string RuleMessage =
+        "A unit test may not read the repository checkout: it depends on the environment, so it is an integration "
+        + "test. Tag the test method or its class [Trait(\"Category\", \"Integration\")], or read a fixture copied "
+        + "to the test output.";
+
+    private static readonly Lazy<string> Located = new(Locate, LazyThreadSafetyMode.ExecutionAndPublication);
+
+    /// <summary>The repository root; resolves only for an integration test or its class fixture.</summary>
+    internal static string Resolve()
+    {
+        EnsureIntegration(TestContext.Current.TestCase?.Traits ?? TestContext.Current.TestClass?.Traits);
+        return Located.Value;
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The <c>McpRepositoryRoot</c> build metadata of the test assembly (TR-MCP-USECASE-018: the deterministic
+    /// repository-root anchor), or null when the project does not embed it; available only for an integration test
+    /// or its class fixture, since the value names the checkout.
+    /// </summary>
+    internal static string? BuildMetadataRoot
+    {
+        get
+        {
+            EnsureIntegration(TestContext.Current.TestCase?.Traits ?? TestContext.Current.TestClass?.Traits);
+            return ReadBuildMetadata();
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>A path under the repository root; resolves only for an integration test or its class fixture.</summary>
+    /// <param name="segments">Path segments below the root.</param>
+    internal static string Combine(params string[] segments)
+    {
+        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(segments);
+        return Path.Combine(new[] { Resolve() }.Concat(segments).ToArray());
+    }
+
+    /// <summary>
+    /// The trait policy shared by <see cref="Resolve"/> and its mock tests: passes only when the traits hold
+    /// <c>Category=Integration</c>; otherwise, including when there is no test context, throws naming the rule.
+    /// </summary>
+    /// <param name="traits">The running test case's or test class's traits, or null outside a test.</param>
+    internal static void EnsureIntegration(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, IReadOnlyCollection<string>>? traits)
+    {
+        if (traits is not null
+            && traits.TryGetValue("Category", out var categories)
+            && categories.Contains("Integration", StringComparer.Ordinal))
+        {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        throw new InvalidOperationException(RuleMessage);
+    }
+
+    private static string Locate()
+    {
+        var configured = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("MCP_REPOSITORY_ROOT");
+        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(configured))
+            return Validate(configured, "Configured");
+
+        var buildRoot = ReadBuildMetadata();
+        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(buildRoot))
+            return Validate(buildRoot, "Build-metadata");
+
+        var startingPoints = new[]
+        {
+            Path.GetDirectoryName(typeof(TestRepositoryRoot).Assembly.Location),
+            AppContext.BaseDirectory,
+            Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(),
+        };
+        foreach (var startingPoint in startingPoints.Where(candidate => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate)))
+        {
+            for (var current = new DirectoryInfo(Path.GetFullPath(startingPoint!)); current is not null; current = current.Parent)
+            {
+                if (IsRepositoryRoot(current.FullName))
+                    return current.FullName;
+            }
+        }
+
+        throw new InvalidOperationException(
+            "Repository root was not found from MCP_REPOSITORY_ROOT, build metadata, the test assembly, the test output, "
+            + "or the current directory.");
+    }
+
+    private static string? ReadBuildMetadata() =>
+        typeof(TestRepositoryRoot).Assembly
+            .GetCustomAttributes<AssemblyMetadataAttribute>()
+            .FirstOrDefault(attribute => string.Equals(attribute.Key, "McpRepositoryRoot", StringComparison.Ordinal))
+            ?.Value;
+
+    /// <summary>Validates an explicit root without falling back to another checkout.</summary>
+    private static string Validate(string candidate, string source)
+    {
+        var root = Path.GetFullPath(candidate);
+        if (!IsRepositoryRoot(root))
+            throw new InvalidOperationException($"{source} repository root is invalid: {root}");
+
+        return root;
+    }
+
+    private static bool IsRepositoryRoot(string root)
+    {
+        var gitMarker = Path.Combine(root, ".git");
+        return (File.Exists(gitMarker) || Directory.Exists(gitMarker))
+            && File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "QBrainAi.sln"))
+            && File.Exists(Path.Combine(root, "Directory.Build.props"));
+    }
+}
